# FF3 architecture — what is known

Living document: add facts as they are established, correct them when they turn out wrong,
cite the FF3 address. Unverified guesses belong in `open-items.md`, not here.

## Contents
- Binary and toolchain
- Address map (game code vs libraries)
- Program flow: main loop
- Game-phase system (the module map)
- Engine idioms
- Core systems and their entry points
- Classes (RTTI): the FF3 class map
- Known globals and objects
- Disc and data files

## Binary and toolchain

- `SLES_538.25` (EU, En/Fr/De/Es/It), PS2 EE (MIPS R5900), no symbols, no debug info.
- Built with **GCC 3.x** (`.jcr`, DWARF2 unwinder, libstdc++-v3). FF2's prototype used GCC 2.96-ee, so
  FF2 and FF3 code rarely match byte for byte even when the source is the same.
- Heavily C++: classes with vtables, function-local static singletons, exceptions, `std::string`,
  locale/iostream code linked in. FF2's `.c` files were compiled as C++ too; ReiPan compiles every `.c` as C++.
- Retail build: asserts and FF2's `_fixed_array_verifyrange` bounds checks are compiled out. A few
  error reporters survive with source file names (`B2dCtl.c`, `B2dSpr.c`, `B2dFile.c`, `B2dInp.c`,
  `BpCtl.c`, `BpTex.c`, `album_load.c`, `album_save.c`).
- Ghidra project: FF3 = program `SLES_538.25.bak` (path `/Fatal Frame 3 - EU - Release`),
  FF2 debug = `SLES_523.84` (path `/FF2 February 6 Debug - EU`, 9,559 named functions).
- **gp = 0x3734f0.** Ghidra shows gp-relative globals as `uGpXXXX`/`Gpffffxxxx`; address = 0x3734f0 + the
  signed 16-bit offset (`Gpffffxxxx` -> 0x3734f0 - (0x10000 - xxxx); sys_wrk's cache 0x372890 = gp-0xc60).
  Ghidra does not track gp-relative xrefs, so "no other writer" of an sdata global is not proof.

## Address map

| range | contents |
|---|---|
| `0x100000`-`0x25fd38` | game code (link order mostly follows FF2's object order, with FF3-only modules inserted) |
| `0x25fd38`-`0x262600` | libsupc++ (throw helpers, exception classes, personality routine, pure virtual) |
| `0x262600`-`0x273a30` | game code continued: utility, sound manager, cddat/fileload, IOP comms, image-load helpers |
| `0x273a30`-`0x2ab000` | Sony libraries (libexcep, cdvd `0x273d40`, libdma, devvu0, kernel syscalls `0x276860`+, sif, libgraph, libvu0, libgifpk, libpad, libmc), libgcc soft-float (`0x299xxx`), newlib |
| `0x2ab000`-`0x2bf000` | game singleton getters and template instances (`GetGPhaseCtrl`, `GetSysWrk`, `GetOptionWrk`, ...) |
| `0x2bf000`-`0x2df690` | libstdc++/libsupc++ (strings, locale, streams, RTTI) |
| `0x2df690`-`0x2e90f0` | `.vutext` (VU microcode); `.DVP.overlay` sections are VU1 overlays |

StepSeqInitTwin 0x109f18 and CsrGridInitTwin 0x10aa90 are unreferenced C1/C2 ctor copies. Ghidra bodies had swallowed 0x109f18 (FUN_00109eb0), 0x10a450 (CamLvGet) and 0x10ac90 (CsrGridSetSkip); 0x10afa8 / 0x10b030 (CEditRoot ctors, CDebugEdit) had no function. |
| 0x10bba0..0x10ce28 | `ingame/clear/GameClear.c` | FF3-only, RTTI `CGameClear` (vtable 0x340ff8, EE size 0x10: layout CB2dBase* +4, STEP_SEQ* +8, reward-list bits +0xc; 2026-10-15, verify): phase 42 (0x2a), requested by GAME_CLEAR_ (GameClearRequest 0x10cd98) after GameClearSetMode(difficulty 0..3 / -1, ending) or by EV_MISSION_CLEAR / FAILED (mode 4 / 5). Twelve steps (list 0x34c2a0; twin 0x34c268 for the base ctor 0x10c9f8): missions go to phase 0xb at once; the story plays ending movie 0x15 (0x16 for the other ending) under linked phase 0x43, then OUTGAME_BG_PSS, BGM_04_CLEAR (0xfb1/0xfb2), GAMECLEAR_PHF + language with the item hooks of list 0x34c0e8 (difficulty sprites R Easy..R Nightmare, rank sprites R S..R E by play-time rank 0x10a5d8, stats 0x40f..0x418: msg 0x5b labels, dream count ingame_wrk.b04 + 1, play time copied at construction to bss 0x377fe0, best score, collection count / total), OK -> reward lists omake1_in / omake2_in (rewards 0..5 and 6..9, ClearRewardIsOpen 0x10a640; rows unroll 30 frames apart following the item's y history at bss 0x377ff0 / 0x3781f4, fading over y 280..300), out, phase 0x2b. Its first functions had wrong FF2 names (FurnCtlCheckKakuType, FurnCtlClearAll, FurnCtlFindInit, FurnCtlDeleteDrawFlgAll, FurnCtlGetSize). |
| 0x10ce28..0x10d8b8 | `ingame/map/LightCtl.c` | FF3-only, RTTI `CLightCtl` (vtable 0x341008, EE size 0x7c; 2026-10-15, verify): the blinking ("meimetu" = 明滅) lights. MapPlayStartReset 0x11d478 news one into the CClassPtr<CLightCtl> 0x38e6d0 (ptr 0x38e6d8) and LightCtlLoadReq loads MEIMETU_ECB 0xada (3ddata/room/meimetu.ecb: ECB header {"ecb", 5, num 7, rec size 0x14}, records at +0x20 of 5 floats {level %, rise, hold, fall, low frames}; -1.0 starts a sequence, -2.0 ends the table) into a system-heap buffer; Wrk38e6d8IsReady 0x11d3e8 then LightCtlStart (counts sequences, rand() picks one). Four steps on a STEP_SEQ (+0x14) drive a LERP_F (+0x68) 20 -> level -> hold -> 20 -> low; LightCtlApply 0x10d588 (MapDBuffDraw, 0x1183e0) copies area 0x12's GRA3DLIGHTDATA to bss 0x378400 (LightCtlGetLightData, MapDoorModeNow) and scales diffuse xyz and the +0x5c/+0x60 ranges of lights 3..26 and 30..37 whose name (area data member 0, +0x10 + id * 0x20) holds "meimetu" by lerp.cur / 100. GetLightCtl = sdata gp-0x7e68. Ctor twin 0x10d080 was swallowed by LightCtlStepNext. |
| 0x109f58..0x10a128 | (unassigned@00109dd8) | the step sequencer STEP_SEQ (EE 0x54: 16 step functions, owner +0x40, num +0x44, cur +0x48, jump +0x4c (-1), stop +0x50): StepSeqInit / Jump / Add (zero-terminated list) / Clear / Run (step returns 0 again, 1 next or jump, 2 end, 3 stop, 4 wait; Run returns 1 unless a step continues). Steps get the sequencer and reach their object through owner. Declared in ingame/map/LightCtl.h until the run is defined; 143 call sites. |

The FF2 names MapAnimGetFreeCtl / MapManimSetAnim / MapManimSetMatrixSgdOne / MapDoorRelease / MapDrawGetLightPtr4BuffID2 on 0x117208 / 0x117220 / 0x117238 / 0x1172f0 / 0x1176b0 were wrong matches |
| 0x117720..0x118900 | `ingame/map/MapDraw.c` | FF2 MapDraw.c rewritten (2026-10-16, verify). Light sets [bank][buffer] 0x1610 each: bank 0 bss 0x37a4d0 = the room's light file at its origin (MapDrawInitRoom 0x117cc0: data member 0, member 6 on side 1 when the data PK4 has 7 members; sgdVerifyLightData into MAP_ROOM_DAT.light, gra3dLightDataAddOffsetPosition), bank 1 bss 0x37d0f0 = what the room last drew with (MapDrawSetRoomLight 0x1183e0: lights off, CLightCtl::Apply on bank 0, EffLanternApplyLight 0x164818 (light 1), ambient + apply, light 38 = the player model's fl (spot: direction xyz negated), copy bank 0 -> 1, gra3dGetLightData into bank 1); MapPutSetLight's room kind and MapObjRegist read bank 1 / 0 through MapDrawGetLightPtr4BuffID 0x117b90, which returns the MapDrawPreLight override (sdata 0x36b7f0[2]) first. MapDrawIsEnableFlashlightOnly (sdata 0x36b7f8, initial 1) gates MapDrawSetRoomLight: 1 sets the room's lights (the FF2 name reads backwards). Furniture loads: MapDrawInitFurn 0x117a00 records each obj_pack member with a model file where it lies and loads its default motion (table 0x2efb58, 26 model -> motion file pairs) at the room's load cursor; MapDrawLoadFurnAll 0x117940 loads the area_pos objects not in the obj_pack. MapDrawRoomObjRegist 0x117de8 (MapLoadSetDrawFlg2 show): per area_pos entry pos = origin + entry, rot = DegToRad(x, 360 - y, z); attr 0-3 MapObjRegist + per-number tweaks (0/56/109/111/119 z offset 30, 1/57/110/112/120 put flag 0x10000, 49 draw func PlyrCameraMapObjDraw, 211 MapSpEventPutVanish, 511 z -1000), attr 4 also EffectWaterFlowRegist (801..899, work = (no-801)%10, draw 0x1e9ef8) / EffectLightComeInRegist (900..999, work = SGD, draw 0x1e9fe8 flags 0x8000, first -1), attr 5 doors (door anime PK4, z -3000, flag 8 when 0x119458, 0x119e50 lights, 72 MapSpEventPutAppear; a failed door put returns from the whole function), attr 6 MapEffRegist(type = digits, label = "_no"); then the "_z" suffix (L: flag 0x8000 + first -z, F: 0x400 + first -z, N: z offset z*10); then MapEffRoomRegist, MapLightRegOnBuff, MapObjUpdate(-1), MapObjSetActionRoom, MapEffVisibleUpdate, stat |= 4. MapDrawRooms 0x118650: per drawn buffer gra3dSetDrawParam(on, 1.3) (on unless area 0x26/0x27/0x2a/0x2b, viewfinder, plyr _0e0 bit 0), MapDrawRoom 0x1185b8 (0x203360 world matrix, lights, ModelManager ReqDraw then Draw / DrawAlpha for flags 0x200). MapDrawLightReset 0x118768, MapDrawRelease 0x1187d0, MapDrawDegToRad 0x1187f0 (wrap to +-180, x 3.141592 / 180). FF2's MapDrawRoomOne / MapDrawObj / SGD coordinate helpers are gone |
| 0x118900..0x119348 (+data 0x2efc28..0x2f1488, bss 0x37fd10..0x37fd3c) | `ingame/map/MapFog.c` | FF2 MapFog.c reshaped (2026-10-16, verify). Setting MAP_FOG_DAT 0x10 {float near, far; short min, max (density 0..255); u8 r, g, b, a} -> gra3dSetFog(min, max, near, far) / gra3dSetFogColor / gra3dApplyFog every frame. MapFogDat 0x2f1458 [3] (start, end, in force; init 2000 / 18550 / 50 / 200 / grey 40). Fog table MapFogTbl 0x2efc28, 0xac rows of 0x24 {int id; dat[2]} (MapFogGetTblDat 0x118900); a fog rectangle (RegDat types 13 then 5) stat record holds only that id (+4). Outside every rectangle the area row of area_data.ecb gives the fog (MAP_AREA_DAT.fog[2] at +4, all 59 rows decode), flag 0x2 marks it so the walk is armed once. dat[1] / [0] by the camera-up flag (PLYR_FLG_800000 or PLYR_WRK +0xe0 bit 0). Snap when the camera moved >= 51 on x / z (FF2 701, xyz) or the camera-up state flipped, else a 16-frame walk. The frame is skipped while MapHitRegistCallback (0x119348, returns 0) is non-zero or the player light is on (CharPlayerModel +0x1bc). No sky fade (FF2 MapSkySetAlpha gone). Scripted override MapFogStartFogEv(near, far, r, g, b, min, max) / MapFogEndFogEv. |

| 0x11bf28..0x11cea8 | `ingame/map/MapPut.c` | FF2 MapPut.c rewritten (2026-10-15, verify): pool of 150 MAP_PUT (0x90: buff, first_id, flg, z_num, z_offset, drawn +0x10, visible +0x14, func +0x18, work +0x1c, mat +0x20, mp +0x60, pos +0x64, lit +0x68, c_pos +0x70, mdl +0x80, next / befo / next_draw +0x84..) at bss 0x389260, list sbss gp-0x92c, now-drawing gp-0x928, light-push flag gp-0x924, saved ambient bss 0x38e6c0, draw stop sdata 0x36b86c (MapPutSetDrawStop, player model change), write-only scene-load word 0x36b868. No 25x scale in FF3 matrices (scale, rot x/y/z radians through NormalizeAngle twice, + pos); model puts keep a MAP_OBJ_MDL and their world bounding box (morph handle of mot->ani_ctrl, flipped by data 0x329400 diag(1,-1,-1,1)). Flag kinds (flg & 0x65820): 0x20 player light, 0x800 real light + box cull, 0x1000 prelight, 0x4000 sway furniture (cull, z limit -30000 / -31000; objects 0x26b..0x282 get a +-6.1 y margin, none with the camera up), 0x20000 room reg light, 0x40000 one-shot mmanageReqMdl; 0x400 first / 0x8000 last order by first_id. MapPutDraw(flags) sets the model matrix once a frame (MAP_OBJ_MDL::SetMatrix, drawn), draws visible models with MAP_OBJ_MDL::Draw unless flags & 8, calls func(put, flags). Ghidra names one field off before this session: SetMatrixPtr / GetMatrixPtr were the lit pointer, SetBuffID / GetBuffID the work word, SetMatrix the z offset. 0x11bf08 is MapObjReg.c's _GLOBAL__I_ (so 0x11beb8 'MapObjRegistPhf' is its static-init function) |

| 0x11e820..0x11f668 (+0x2ac870..0x2ac97c, data 0x2f1530) | `ingame/map/MapRcb.c` | FF3-only (2026-10-16, done: the map camera shows the room after the opening event). The room's rcb file = room data PK4 member 3 (MapLoadGetRoomData(dat, 3)): RCB_HEAD 0x10 {"rcb", ?, group_num, group}, RCB_GROUP 0x10 {vtx_num, vtx (vec4, room space), obj_num, obj}, RCB_OBJ 0x40 {name[0x20], short prm[10] +0x20, kind +0x34, quad_num +0x38, quad +0x3c (int[4] vertex indices)}. RcbInit 0x11ec70 (RoomFileSetHit) relocates once (offset <= file address), sets each kind from the name prefix (table 0x2f1530: hit_ 0, se_ 1, pos_ 2, change_ 4, kai_ 3, mot_ 5, tourou_ 6) and prm from its _<n> parts (sscanf %hd), moves vertices by the room origin with y * 0, w 1. RcbForEachObj 0x11ea28 (kind or -1, prm[idx] == val or idx < 0, cb(group, obj, arg) nonzero stops; -1 not rcb, -2 not relocated) / RcbForEachQuad 0x11ec38 (cb(obj, 4 vertex pointers, arg)); queries hand a 0x40 stack block to their callbacks. Meanings: kai_<mh>_<f> = hit area mh is on floor 11 + f (RcbGetFloor 0x11f578, 11 when none; the RegDat files' 11 / 13 / 15); change_<mh>_<new> zones switch the player's hit area (RcbUpdateMhNo 0x11f5f0, PlyrMapPosUpdate); se_<sound>_<floor>_<prio> footstep areas (RcbGetSeObj 0x11f2c0 lowest prio under pos, RcbGetSeNoList 0x11f3b8 up to 8); mot_<mh>_<type>_<dir> player motion zones (RcbGetMotObj 0x11f4b8, up to 2: PlyrOnMapObj / PlyrGetMapObjDir); RcbPushOut 0x11f028 (MapHitPushOutQuad per quad), RcbLineHit 0x11f158 (nearest edge crossing, quad test RcbLineHitQuad 0x2ac870 linked late); RcbGetFloorMhNoList 0x11ef58 is unreferenced. The FF2 matches MhCtlGetMapHeight / MhCtlHitLineCheck at 0x11ea28 / 0x11eb68 were wrong (MapCtrl.c holds FF2 MhCtl's height wrappers) |

Branch ops (0xa0..0xd4) jump on: facing range, hp percent / bars (EneHpRateCeil), XZ or 3D distance, anime flags, loop timers, dmg_react, chance, the attack hit check (B0D, flags 0x100000000 / 0x200000000 = motion script op 0xd / 0x10 windows with ENE_WRK _6a8 reach / _6ac angle), the hold (B0E), battle time, linked enemies' state / act / var (B16..B19), act_req (B1E), the view cone (B21), the player's flags2 / status / mode, the candle (B2A), an arm sweep (B2C, LineIntersect), the tattoo event state (B33 / B34). Effect ops: E00 CallNega2, E01 EffLanternCut(-1), E02 / E03 grey fade in / out, E04 / E05 the tattoo event (TattooEvStart / Resume, ingame/tattoo_event.c), E06 / E07 the object-effect rate. Control ops: C00 jump, C01 wait u8 (PAL-scaled), C02 jump after a frame, C03 end of action (flags 0x80008: EneActRule 0x18a808 restarts act 2 / 1), C04 anime, C05 / C06 loop timers, C07 hit anime by dmg_react, C08 flag 0x800000000, C09 / C0A call / return, C0B / C0C attacks (EneAttackReq), C0D flag 0x800000, C0E / C0F fade alpha_a and the deform rates (flag 0x400000, per-slot bss 0x3df2c0..0x3df440). Blink: L00 wait, L01 jump, L02 skip 4, L03 / L04 jump on flags 0x400000 / 0x800000, L05 the alpha wobble, L06 nop. Tables: blink 0x3013a0 (7 + null, FF2 BCommJmpTbl), control 0x3013c0 (ops 0x00..0x6f, 72 entries), move 0x3014e0 (0x70.., 14), branch 0x301518 (0xa0.., 54; entry 0x35 null), effect 0x3015f0 (0xe0.., 8: E00..E07, FF2 had 6). Operands are LE pairs lo + hi*0x100; jumps are offsets from +0x00. All 154 table entries are Ghidra functions named by slot (BJobLnn, EJobCnn / Mnn / Bnn / Enn, 2026-10-19). The old FF2 names in this run (EnemyEffectPosUpdate, EnemyPhotoMain, PreloadedEneAllRelease, EneAllRelease, CheckEneDepth, SetAtkTarget, ChangeAtkTargetRnd, SetEneSlowMode / Clear, EneMotAlgCtrl 0x191380) are wrong matches: they are opcode handlers |

Two ROM tables name every handler by its script keyword (`python evtbl.py cmd|cond`): commands 0x3556b8 (235 rows of 0x30: id, handler, argument bytes, format, name[0x20]; MOVIE_PLAY 0x19e538 .. Format: first digit = bytes to skip, then one 1/2/4-byte signed argument per digit, decoded by 0x19b158 into a 0x80-byte int array the dispatcher points EV_EXE_CTRL +0xc at (+8 = the command); handlers return 1 when done. All 230 handlers and 71 conditions carry Ev+keyword names (FF2 names where FF2 has the keyword); 81 + 14 of them were hidden inside neighbours and were mkfunc'd. Written (partial): AreaChange 0x19f110 (lock ids), effect save blocks 0x19fb18..0x19fcb8 (ev_save_screen_effect 0x3730e8 {fade_lock, eff_number}, ev_save_eff_dither 0x3730e0 6 bytes, dither ids 0x3730d8, synchro_mode_flg 0x370254), sound / stream control 0x1a2fb0..0x1a3c60 (ev_sound_ctrl 0x3e6850 30 x 0xc keyed by SE id; ev_stream_ctrl 0x3e6a20 2 x 0xc {id, file, state 1 play / 2 preload}; ev_save_stream 0x3e69c0 2 x 0x30, FF2's three saved-stream tables merged with a type byte 0 2D / 1 object / 2 position, save block 12) with the SOUND_* / STREAM_* / MAP_STREAM_* / BG_SE_STOP commands, EvStreamPlaySub 0x2b8f70, GetFinderBankWrk 0x2b8f28 (0x424b00 is the wakeup / sleep movie pair of SET_WAKEUP_MOVIE / SET_SLEEP_MOVIE, not a finder bank). EV_EXE_CTRL (FF3): +0 event id, +4 EV_STOP timer, +8 command, +0xc args, +0x12 handler step, +0x13 if state (1 taken, 2 skipped, 3 done), +0x14 the condition's byte size (EvExeGetWord0c). Handler sdata: 0x370234 MSG_DISP anim slot, 0x370238 DISP2D anim, 0x370244 / 0x370248 MSG_CHOICE anim and answer, 0x37023c / 0x370240 0.01 (camera roll / fov scale), 0x37024c 4.8 (SET_PLAYER_HIT). Lock-id tables: 0x358318 (15 ids, CALL_GHOST_SEAL_DOOR_BURN), 0x358358 (14 ids, GO_DREAM_WORLD / GO_REAL_WORLD). The executor pause byte 0x3e1c74 (EvExeSetPause / EvExeIsPause) is set while a message window is open; EV_STOP does not count then. INGAME_WRK b01 = CHANGE_CHAPTER's byte, b02 = chapter (CHAPTER_DISP_START), b03 = the world (EV_CHANGE_WORLD; PLYR_FLASHLIGHT_SET does nothing at 1), b04 = a count GO_DREAM_WORLD raises to 98 and chapter 1 clears, _24 the item table (ITEM_TABLE_CHANGE_ / ITEM_RESET), _28 passed on by SHOOT_DEVELOP_FILM. Session 3 wrote every remaining command handler 0x1a07a0..0x1a2df0 and phase 36 Story_RoomReload (0x1a2e40 / 0x1a2ee0 / 0x1a2eb8; EvRoomReloadReq 0x1a2df8 keeps area / map hit object / return phase in bss 0x3e6840 / 44 / 48; SET_HAICHI_ID on the loaded area asks for it). World switch (GO_DREAM_WORLD / GO_REAL_WORLD, PLYR_MODEL_CHANGE): steps 0 locks -> 4 one frame -> 1 switch -> 2 wait; EvPlyrMdlChangeStart 0x1a1f58 / EvPlyrMdlChangeWait 0x1a1fd0 around PlyrMdlChange with lock 6 and the player data file 0xadb + no (FUN_001f4680); MapAreaSideGet 0x1f2228 == 1 is the night / real side. Event NPC numbers: 0 / 1 the player-camera characters (n_plyr_camera.c 0x21f0a0..), 2 and up the cats (cat_ctrl.c, number - 2). Map object rows are FURN_SAVE_ROW (ingame/map/FurnSave.h): FurnSaveFind 0x1fa3d8(area, kind 0 effect / 1 furniture / 2 door, id, label). Handler sdata 0x370270 FILE_READ's anim slot, 0x370274 the player mode PLYR_MOTION_CALL left (mode 9 is the event motion mode). The phases EV_MISSION_START 31, DEVELOP_REQUEST 0x26 (38), PUZZLE_START 49, EV_MISSION_CLEAR / FAILED 50. Session 4 (2026-10-15) finished the file (324 / 324, status verify): SET_BGM_LIST, TAPE_START (cassette menu 0x25b790 / 0x25b810 / 0x25ba68), the TEL_* commands (ambient SE work 0x3fd0e8), EV_SET / ADD / SUB_WORK on EV_EXE_CTRL +0x10 (a short; EV_WORK_CHECK finds the running ctrl with EvExeGetCtrl 0x19af58: 0x18-byte slots 0x3e31e8..0x3e3ff8 keyed by event id) and every condition 0x1a3e38..0x1a4af0. Conditions take (event id, the condition's bytes) and memcpy the bytes into a stack struct laid out by the format; the comparing ones use an operator byte 8 >=, 9 <=, 10 == (EV_CNT_EXEC, EV_WORK_CHECK, EV_NOW_CHAPTER on INGAME_WRK b01). FINDER_MODE_WRK +0 / +4: CHECK_MISSION's group * 5 + number. The rect (sdata gp-0x188 maxX, -0x184 minX, -0x180 maxY, -0x17c minY, -0x178 min view z, -0x174 max) is padded a pixel and clamped to the window (1728, 1824) 640x448. Scissor = the rect; afterwards a Z-only sprite (TEST 0x32001: alpha NEVER, AFAIL ZB_ONLY, Z ALWAYS, Z 0) clears the rect's depth and the scissor returns to pdrawenv1->scissor1. OK?' 0x77/2 type 1), 96 PzlKaza_Turn (init: Step until no ring turns, solved -> label "out" (sdata 0x371f80), wait BpCtlIsLoadEnd; one: solved -> PuzzleClear, SE 0x1bcb0002, 0x77/4; out of moves -> 0x77/3; else 95); GPhaseCtrl atexit thunk 0x24aca8. kaza_draw.c 0x24acc8..0x24af48 = board defs data 0x361da0 (2 x 0x54: rest 4 / 5, marks[5][4]; board 0's only solution is ring 0 twice, rings 1 and 2 once), item tables data 0x361e48 (cursor frames 1012 1029 1023 1026, 1026) / 0x361e58 (rings 1020 1017 1019 1018 1016, turned by B2dSprSetRot with part 16 frozen), moves digits 0x3ea..0x3f2, button guide 0x3f5 (hidden under phase 12). kaza_board.c 0x24af48..0x24b490 = KAZA_RING (0xc: pos, float angle, mark table; Step: 2 deg x frame scale toward pos x 90 the short way, wrapped in 0..360) and KAZA_BOARD (0x44: ring[5], rest +0x3c, cursor +0x40; ctor copies 0x24b158 / 0x24b1b8). 0x24b408 / 0x24b460 were the wrong FF2 match SavePointFadeInMsgDispPad and an unnamed neighbour; 0x24b490 (a MenuCsr filter) starts the sizumedama code |

| 0x271cb8..0x272d90 | `system/playpss/playpss.c` | FF2 playpss.o (2026-10-14, MioPan port, verify), 23 functions: playPssInit..fillBuff in FF2 order (vblankHandler 0x2720f0, playPssIsPause/Pause/Restart 0x2725f0/0x272608/0x272660, audioCallback 0x272838, backgroundCallback 0x272a18, errorCallback 0x272a88 were swallowed by their neighbours) plus FF3-only playPssSetTexPacket 0x272b38 (decode into a texture chain via setLoadTexTags 0x273d08 at tbp; the chain size is kept in sdata gp-0xcb4 from the first frame; GetPicture gets (w/16)*(h/16) macroblocks). Statics: flags gp+0x5d8 (short stop phase 0/1/2, then bits bStarted 0, bPauseFlg 1, bAudioRun 2 (FF3: the PAL thread pauses/resumes audio itself), bNtsc2Pal 3, bSendImage 4, bErrorCallback 5), gp+0x5dc..0x610 thread/read func/bgDuration/mux and demux ring/rsrcs/audio flag, AudioDec 0x413700 (0x60), sceMpeg 0x413760, thread stack 0x411700 (0x2000), sdata gp-0xcc4..-0xca8 PAL counter/VT counter/tex size/sema/vblank handle. FF3 libmpeg: sceMpegDemuxPss(mp, buf, size) takes a size (FF2 did not); sceMpegGetPicture 0x289558, Reset 0x2895f8, IsEnd 0x289678, AddCallback 0x2896d0, AddStrCallback 0x28db50, Delete 0x289510 (return 1). PC: the PAL thread is never armed (no vblank INTC), as in MioPan |
| 0x273568..0x273d40 | `system/playpss/ldimage.c` | FF2 ldimage.o (2026-10-15, MioPan port, done): setLoadImageTags 0x273568 -> setLoadImageTagsYX 0x2735a0 (PATH3 chain: CNT + PACKED A+D BITBLTBUF (dbw 10) / TRXREG 16x16 head, per macroblock in column order CNT + TRXPOS + TRXDIR + IMAGE GIFtag + REF to 0x400 bytes, END), static builders setDMAscTag 0x273818, setGIFtag 0x273880, setGIFad 0x2738d0, setBITBLTBUF 0x2738f8, setTRXPOS 0x273948, setTRXREG 0x273998, setTRXDIR 0x2739d0, loadImage 0x2739f8 (GIF channel TADR / QWC 0 / CHCR 0x105); FF3-only setVIFcodes 0x273a30 (two words into a tag's upper half), setLoadTexTagsYX 0x273a40 (the same chain for the VIF1 packet buffer: every tag carries VIF NOP + DIRECT n, BITBLTBUF dbw (width+63)/64, no END tag, returns the size in bytes: 0x40 + 0x60 per macroblock, 26944 for 320x224) and setLoadTexTags 0x273d08 (stride 0x400; caller playPssSetTexPacket). Ghidra's ff2? names here were shifted one function and 0x273a30 was a wrong sceExcepConsClose match (renamed). PC: both entry points arm the direct picture path (reipan_video.h) at their GS block; the chains are built but not walked while it is armed |

FF2's eecdvd functions (LoadReq, LoadReqGetAddr, IsLoadEndAll, IsLoadEnd) sit just before system.c at
0x259680..0x2597f0. FF3 has no main.c at FF2's link slot after m_plyr_camera.c. FF2's order
`option.c` -> `pad_check.c` -> `pause.c` survives, with FF3's pad layer inserted after option.c (FF2's
pad driver `system/pad/pad.c` is elsewhere, ~0x1f0138). Known but undefined runs are listed in
open-items.md "Boundaries seen, not defined".

Library code is replaced on PC; name it only when game code needs it. `reipan.LIB_RANGES` encodes the
library ranges (plus `0x100000`-`0x100388`, crt0); `tus.py` and `status.py` never queue or count them.

## Program flow

```
crt0 entry 0x100134 -> _init, atexit, main(argc, argv)
main 0x25a3e8 (in system/os/system.c, not main.c):
    InitMainSystem() 0x25a450             InitSystemHeap, packet-buffer halves, InitSystemON (IOP boot
                                          only: ee_iopInit with IOPRP300/ZEROIRX/ZERO3.FHD/IMG_BD.BIN),
                                          McInit, InitGraphics (forces PAL, InitGsRegCache, gra3dInit),
                                          InitVBlank, PadInitAll, srand(RCNT0), RCNT0/1 modes
    loop:
        GameSystemStart() 0x1d2640        per session: phase tree, GPhaseCtrlInit/SetNext(0)/Change,
                                          sound/movie/option/fade-layer/B2d inits
        do GameSystemFrame() 0x1d2610     GPhaseCtrlMain(GetGPhaseCtrl()) once per frame
        until CheckSoftReset/IsSoftResetCountOver (pad 0 holds L1+L2+R1+R2+START+SELECT 60 frames)
        sys->sreset_count = 0; GameSystemEnd() 0x1d2810, then the outer loop restarts
per frame, after_super -> SendDMAMain 0x25a638:
    CheckVif1Stall, WaitVBlank (vblank sema, SetVBlankWaitNum, default 2), sys->count++,
    SwapDBuff (sceGsSwapDBuffDc + SetPCRTC + draw-env pointers), PacketBufKick
```

FF2's vfunc is split into WaitVBlank + SwapDBuff. Video modes (`SYS_WRK.video_mode`): 2 NTSC, 3 PAL,
0x50 480p; InitSysWrk defaults to NTSC but InitGraphics switches to PAL at boot, ChangeVideoMode 0x25a0b8
(title menu) changes it. `GetFrameTimeScale` 0x25a040 returns 1.2 in PAL, 1.0 otherwise (57 callers).

EE memory map set up at boot: packet buffers 0x440000/0x640000 (2 x 0x200000), C++ heap 0x840000
(0x20000), IOP heap 0x860000 (0x80000, `GetIopHeapWrkP` 0x1e7570, serves ee_iop's malloc64/free64),
gra3d packet area 0x8e0000 (0x10000), system heap 0x137c100 (0xc23f00, `GetSystemHeapWrkP` 0x1e7560).

## Game-phase system

FF2's GPhase design rebuilt as C++ (`GPhaseCtrl*`, `GPhaseTree*`, `GPhaseNode*`): 120 phases. Code in
`src/main/gphase.c` (controller, nodes, tables) and `src/main/gphase_tree.c`; types in their headers.

- Function tables, 120 x 4 bytes: init `0x3275c0`, one `0x3277a0`, end `0x327980`, pre `0x327b60`,
  after `0x327d40`. Tree `0x327f20` (0x3c0 bytes, copied to the heap by GPhaseTreeInit): 8 bytes per
  phase = {depth, parent, first child, child count}; entry 0 = {0, -1, 1, 12}.
- Controller `GPHASE_CTRL` (bss 0x41e468, 0x10): shorts `from` (phase whose one asked for the last change),
  `now`, `next` (-1 none), `next_link`; node pointers `leaf` (deepest) and `root` (super's node).
- Nodes (`GPHASE_NODE`, operator new, 0x14): parent, child, id, state, `state_cnt` (+0xc short),
  `state_wrk` (+0x10 int). States: 0 init (until init returns non-zero), 1 load wait (FF3's hook always
  returns 1), 2 run (pre on the way down, the leaf's one, after on the way up), 3 end wait (always 1),
  4 end (until end returns non-zero), 5 done (popped by GPhaseCtrlFreeEndedNodes).
- Per frame (`GPhaseCtrlMain`): `GPhaseCtrlBuildStack` pushes one level per frame while the leaf runs
  and is a strict ancestor of `now`; a running node ends when its phase is off `now`'s root path
  (`GPhaseTreeIsOffPath`). `one` != 0 applies `GPhaseCtrlChange` at once: from = leaf phase; with the
  link flag `now` is re-parented under `from` (`GPhaseTreeLink`, depths renumbered) — how sub-phases
  return to their caller. `next` is cleared when the requested phase finishes its load wait.
- **Phase step idiom**: multi-frame init/one functions switch on `GetGPhaseCtrl()->leaf->state_cnt` and
  advance it themselves (`state_wrk` likewise); the engine clears both on every state change.
- `GPhaseCtrlSetNext(ctrl, id, link)` sets the next phase. `GPhaseCtrlRequest(id, link)` (ingame.c)
  arbitrates against a pending request through an 18-entry priority table at 0x359f08 and returns the
  phase actually requested. Many sub-phases return to a phase stored in a variable (e.g. pause's
  `pause_ret_phase` = ctrl->from), so transitions are not all constants.
- Phase functions are named `init_/one_/end_/pre_/after_<Phase>` (root: `*_super`), FF2's convention.
  init/one/end are `int(void)`, pre/after `void(void)`. Tools: `phases.py` (dump; `--c` writes
  `gphase_ff3_func` for the PC fallback), `phasegraph.py` (constant transitions), `data/phases.json`.
- **PC**: table slots of phases not written yet are empty; `GPhaseCallMissing` prints
  `[stub] phase<N>_<kind> (FF3 0x...) reached` so the smoke run ranks that phase's TU; init/end then
  return 1 and one returns 0 (stay). A written phase TU adds its functions to the five tables in
  `src/main/gphase.c` (prototypes from its own header).

## Engine idioms

- **Singletons**: `if (!inst) inst = GetX(); inst->Method()` everywhere; getters at `0x2ab000`+ construct a
  static object once and register its destructor with `atexit` (objects: sys_wrk 0x41e4b0 via `GetSysWrk`
  0x2abdf8, GPhaseCtrl 0x41e468, OptionWrk 0x41e580, MapSky 0x41e4c8, PacketBufMgr 0x41e478 via
  `GetPacketBufMgr` 0x2ab428, `GetGPhaseTree`). The cached pointer is one gp-relative global shared by all
  TUs (e.g. sys_wrk's at 0x372890: an inline function's static). Methods take the object as first
  argument (`GetLanguage(sys)`, `CheckSoftReset(sys)`); the getter calls the constructor (`InitSysWrk`).
- **TU ends**: the `atexit` destructor thunks of those statics (`GPhaseCtrlTerm(&ctrl)` wrappers at
  0x1d06f0, 0x1d25c8, 0x1d29f8, 0x1d4640, 0x2245f8, 0x225498; `GPhaseTreeTerm` 0x1d4210) are emitted at
  the end of an object — a reliable file boundary. They do not say which getters the file uses
  (gphase_tree.c emits a GPhaseCtrl thunk without calling GetGPhaseCtrl). Likewise GCC's static-init
  pair — `__static_initialization_and_destruction_0` (called with init == 1, prio == 0xffff) and the
  `_GLOBAL__I_*` that calls it — closes its object (0x13e5d0 in cmn_fade_layer.c; 0x13e890/0x13ef80
  for cmn_caption.c after it). Every `_GLOBAL__I_*` is listed in `.ctors` (0x2e90f4.., one word per
  object with static constructors, in link order), so the table is a list of file ends: read it with
  the ELF, not Ghidra (`0x2e90f4`: 0x109d98, 0x10d898, 0x10e610, 0x1188b0, 0x11af80, 0x11bf08,
  0x11d608, 0x139fd0, 0x13b300, 0x13e668, 0x13ef80, 0x1517d0, 0x15b328, 0x180680, ...).
- **C++ code shape** (GCC 3, Itanium ABI): vtable slots follow declaration order, and a virtual
  destructor takes two slots (complete, deleting) *where it is declared* — first in most FF3 classes,
  but `GameModel`'s are at +8/+0xc. EE vtable offset / 4 = slot. Base/complete constructor pairs are
  byte-identical copies. Inline dtors, empty virtuals and template instances land in the 0x2ab000+
  area; one-instruction virtuals (0x1d0160, 0x1d04c8) and some dtors (0x2ad408) are not Ghidra
  functions — read the vtable (`rtti.py --class`).
- **Classes are recoverable from RTTI** (`tools/reverse/rtti.py`, see "Classes" below): every
  polymorphic FF3 class has its real name, base, vtable and ctors/dtors in the binary. Use those names;
  FF2's C structs often came back as these classes ("objectified"), with free functions turned into
  methods taking `this` first.
- **Init/Main/Draw trio**: subsystems expose `XInit` / `XMain` / `XDraw` (or `XEnd`), called from
  IngameInit / IngameMain / IngameDrawSub (Story_Main) or a phase's init/one.
- **Layouts (B2d/Bp)**: FF3-only 2D system. Bp files are layout packs loaded by file number into a 45-entry
  table (`BpCtlLoadReq`, `BpCtlIsLoadEnd` [really "label animation ended"], `BpCtlGetHandleByFileNo`,
  `BpCtlRelease`, `BpCtlMain` every frame); animations play by label string (`BpPlayLabel(handle,
  "START_in")`); items/sprites via `B2dCtl*`/`B2dSpr*`. Language-specific files are `base + GetLanguage()`.
  - Bp table (`system/b2d/BpCtl.c`): 45 `BP_CTL_ENT` at bss 0x377730 (0x24: handle, file (-1 free), load
    id, buf, b2d, work, tex, flags: low nibble state 1 load / 2 ready / 3 play / 4 ended, +0x10 pause,
    +0x100 user buffer; draw). State steps table 0x2ef3a0 {1 BpCtlStepLoad 0x107410, 3 BpCtlStepPlay
    0x107568, 4 BpCtlStepEnd 0x1075c0}. A Bp file is a .b2d or a PHF pack (`ingame/map/MapPhf.c`: head "phf\0", size, num, data_ofs;
    0x20-byte `PHF_FILE` entries name[0x18], ofs, size from +0x10) whose `*.b2d` member (`_phf_.b2d`) is the
    layout and whose `.tm2` members are its textures (BpTex looks them up by name, PhfFindFileData). Load buffers: files 0x1245..0x1249 /
    0x124a..0x124e at the tail of packet-buffer half 0/1, 0x120f..0x1227 and 0x12e3..0x12e7 in a bump arena
    (BpArenaSet 0x1064e8, sdata 0x36b57c/0x36b580), others on the system heap.
  - B2d core (`system/b2d/B2dCtl.c`, types in `B2dCtl.h`): entry table 0x373f40 (45 x 0x34 `B2D_ENTRY`:
    item array, key pool, transform x/y/sx/sy/alpha, item list). An item (`B2D_ITEM`, EE 0x1c8: 0x18
    header {+2 kind, +4 id, +8 name, +0xc/+0x10 hooks, +0x14 next} + 18 parts of 0x18) has a kind (0
    sprite, 1 rect, 2/3 hook-drawn quads; bit 4 hidden). Parts (`B2D_PART_NO`): 3 w, 4 h, 5 parent id,
    6 x, 7 y, 8/9 scale (0x400 = 1.0), 10 alpha, 11 text id (unconfirmed); each animated by a chain of
    0x10-byte keys from the entry pool (ease in flags & 0xf; loop start 0x200 / loop end 0x100). Items are
    made by `B2dCtlAdd{Sprite,Rect,Hook,Group}Item` (0x102ad0..0x102e00; a kind-3 item is its own parent
    and groups the items naming it). `B2dCtlDraw(handle, frame)` steps and draws every item through the
    draw hooks at sdata 0x36b528..0x36b540 (gp-0x7fc8..: sprite, rect, quad, flat quad, two no-ops); the
    sprite hook doubles as the "B2d initialised" flag. Alpha/rgb bytes 0x80 = 1.0. Texture-set hook
    gp-0x9e8 (0x372b08), error print callback 0x36b54c. All B2d callbacks use the `B2D_HOOK_ARGS` ABI.
  - .b2d files (`system/b2d/B2dFile.c`, types in `B2dFile.h`): head {magic "b2d", +4 command offset,
    +8 string table offset (0x10 in retail files), +0xc command count}; commands {type, count, count
    words}, strings by offset. A label = command 1 (name, reset-keys flag, B2dInp wait frames) .. command 2;
    `_RegistSprite_` creates the items (BpCtlRegistSprite), the others key them (BpPlayLabel ->
    B2dFilePlayLabelEx). Commands: 0 texture-load hook (0x372b0c, file name), 3 delete, 4..7 create sprite /
    rect / hook / group item (parent in part 5), 16 select the item, 8..13, 17, 18 add keys (xform, pos,
    scale, alpha, rot, rgb, text, repeat), 14 anchor, 15 no-op, 0x13..0x24 bind B2dInp event n to a label.
    Per handle (0x2e9218, 45 x 0x224): data, 128 label pointers, selected id/item, 18 part flag bytes
    (1 reset keys on the next key, 2 re-arm 1 on select, 4 lockable: a key flagged 0x400 locks the part, 8
    locked; PlayLabelEx runs with 2|4). Work size = items * 0x1c8 + biggest label's keys * 0x10.
  - Input (`system/b2d/B2dInp.c`): per handle (`B2D_INP`, 0x374868) btn_arg, wait and 18 labels; events
    = {label, mask} table 0x2ef308 (labels 0..7 directions, diagonals first; 8..17 buttons 0x10..0x2000).
    The direction hook gp-0x7fa0 (BpPadGetDir8) gets the wait; two more hooks gp-0x7f9c/-0x7f98 are never
    set. `B2dInpUpdate`, `B2dInpSetBtnArg`, `B2dInpGetWait`, `B2dInpEnd` have no callers in retail.
  - Sprite parts (`system/b2d/B2dSpr.c`): `B2dSin/B2dCos` (0x400 = turn, 0x1000 = 1.0; tables built by
    B2dTrigInit from sdata 0x36b560 = 2pi/1024 with newlib sin/cos 0x2945c8/0x294480), `B2dRotatePoints`
    (part 16 is degrees). Part flags bit 0 = frozen: B2dItemStep and the rewind skip it, so game code can
    drive a part itself (`B2dSprFreezePart/ByName`, `B2dSprFreezeItem` = parts 6..17). Item setters
    `B2dSprSet{Disp,Pos,Scale,Alpha,Text,Repeat,Anchor,Rot,Rgb}` write part values directly.
  - Direct GS draws (`system/b2d/BpCtl.c`, `B2dDraw{Sprite,Rect,Quad}Direct`): coordinates
    (v + 0x6c0 / 0x720) * 16, GIF tags in sdata 0x36b588/590/5a0/5d8, blend flags 0x10 add / 0x20 sub
    (`BpGetBlendAlpha`). Primitives (PRIM in the tags): sprite / B2dNum glyph = SPRITE 0x1d6 (TME ABE
    FST, Z 100), rect = SPRITE 0x46 (flat), quad = **TRIFAN** 0x155 (TME), flat quad = TRIFAN 0x145; the
    quad hooks get B2dItemGetQuad's perimeter order TL, BL, BR, TR. Texture state: TEX0_1 from the last
    BpTex packet, TEX1_1 / CLAMP_1 / TEST_1 / ZBUF_1 from `BpSetDrawGsRegs` (cached). On PC each hook
    also draws a renderer quad (BpHostTexQuad / BpHostFlatQuad; TEX0 shadowed by `BpTexGetHostTex0`).
    B2dNum font: bss 0x377d88 (0xcc: flags, handle, tex, 12 glyphs u v w h; 10 separator, 11 minus).
  - Textures (`system/b2d/BpTex.c`, types in `BpTex.h`): slot table bss 0x377e58 (45 `BP_TEX_WORK *`,
    one per Bp handle that came from a pack; BpCtlLoadTex mallocs `num * 0x18 + 0x28`). Work {handle,
    clut_tbp, tbp, num, 0x18-byte entries {id (-1 free), tbp, u_long TEX0, tim2}}; BpCtl passes tbp 0x2bc0
    for every handle. B2dFile command 0 -> `BpTexLoadFunc` (a0 handle, a1 texture number, a2 name):
    finds "<name>.tm2" (23 chars max) in `BpGetLoadPack()` and rewrites the picture's GsTex0 in place:
    TBP0 = tbp + 0x82, CBP = tbp, TBW = (1 << TW) / 64, TCC 1, CLD 1. So all of a handle's textures share
    one VRAM spot and are re-sent whenever the B2d core switches texture: `BpTexSetFunc` (texture-set
    hook) -> `MakeTim2SendPacket_3Dpkt(tim2, 0, -1, -1)` then TEXFLUSH + TEX0_1 by direct DMA.
    `BpTexSetClut(handle, id, tim2)` swaps in another TIM2's clut at clut_tbp.
  - TIM2 sends (`graphics/graph2d/tim2.c`, FF3 0x25c300..0x25cc20, written 2026-10-13; Tim2CalcBufWidth 0x25c300 added 2026-10-19): every sender takes
    (tim2, offset, tbp/cbp) — `offset` is added to the TBP0/CBP in the first picture's GsTex0, a value
    >= 0 replaces it. `Tim2GetFirstPicture` 0x25c368 (+0x10, or +0x80 when byte 5 is 1),
    `MakeTim2Direct` 0x25c440 (image, TEX0's TBW/PSM), `MakeClutDirect` 0x25c5d0 (CT32, DBW 1, 8x2 for 16
    colours else 16 x n/16), `MakeClutDirect_ChrMono` 0x25c738 (tim2, mono, offset, cbp), PK2SendVram(One)
    0x25c8c0/0x25c960, `_3Dpkt` 0x25ca00, `_Mono` 0x25ca48, `PK4SendVram` 0x25caa8, `Tim2PackSend` 0x25cb60
    (one member). Each writes a TEXFLUSH/BITBLTBUF/TRXPOS/TRXREG/TRXDIR A+D packet then
    `SetDmaDirectImageTrans` 0x1cadf0 (GIF IMAGE tag + DMA REF to the texels). PC: nothing walks the DMA
    chain, so the senders also hand the texels to `ReiPan_GsUpload` (MioPan's Tim2HostUpload pattern).
  - Packs (`common/pk4.c`, 0x1eff18..0x1f0138): PK2 = count +0, offsets +0x10 (`Pk2GetData`); PK4 =
    "PK4\0" +0, headed flag +4, count +8, offsets +0x10 (`Pk4GetData` 0x1f0098 adds the member's header
    size when headed: {head_size, size, name}). LANG_SL_PK4 is unheaded with three 256x256 TIM2s
    (PSMT8/PSMT4/PSMT8, TBP 11200/11456/11584).
- **Menu cursors** (`ingame/menu/menu_csr.h` over `common/csr_ctrl.h`, reconstructed 2026-10-14 and
  checked against the ROM code on mipsemu): `MenuCsrInit(id, w, h, cfg)` makes a w x h grid whose
  cell k holds item k (table from the system heap; `MenuCsrInitWithFilter(func, ...)` lets
  func(tbl, w, h) write -1 into disabled items; `MenuCsrInitTbl` uses a fixed ROM layout, not freed);
  `MenuCsrUpdate(id, pad)` per frame; "pos" is the **item id** under the cursor (`MenuCsrGetPos/SetPos`),
  `MenuCsrSetViewX/Y(id, size, margin)` + `MenuCsrGetTopX/Y` / `GetPosX/Y` give the scroll window
  (-1 when no view); `MenuCsrIsMovedSe(id, se)` = flag 0x1000 this frame. cfg bits: 1 clamp y, 2 clamp
  x (else wrap), 4 L1/R1 and 8 L2/R2 reported as event bits 0x100/0x400 and 0x200/0x800 (CSR_EV_*).
  Input is `pad_wrk[pad].held & 0xf00f`: a new press acts at once, held repeats after 10 then every
  5 frames; a step passes over -1 cells and cells of the same item. FF3 bug kept: `CsrItemSetXY`
  0x1ee638 tests x < h and reads tbl[x*y] (then rereads the right cell). Degenerate tables (row 0
  all -1, some clamp/bounce layouts) make the ROM loop forever; real menus do not hit them.
- **Layout animations** (`ingame/menu/anim_2d.h`): 8 slots of 0x28 (`LAYOUT_ANIM`) at 0x3919e8.
  `LayoutAnimStart(type, arg)` returns a slot (-1 none free); the type selects the Bp file and the
  pad/draw callbacks (anicode.c; type 4 = pause menu, choice word 0x3918e8 via PauseMenuGetSel/SetSel;
  type 0xc = pad-error message). Callers poll `LayoutAnimGetState`:
  1 started, 2 shown, 3 closing, 4 done (choice final); `LayoutAnimIsStateChanged` = state != state_old.
  `LayoutAnimMain` each frame. The message window (`LayoutMsgSet(msg0, msg1)`, `SetType`, `SetArg`)
  keeps its state at 0x3919b0..0x3919e0. `LayoutAnimStart` pushes `pad_wrk[0].stick_dpad = 1` on a byte
  stack (0x391870, depth gp-0x5a88; `PadStickDpadPush/Pop`), `LayoutAnimReset` pops it.
- **Message printer** (`graphics/graph2d/message.h`, 0x20c400..0x20df28): messages are byte streams in
  MSG_OBJ + language (file 0xad1..0xad5) loaded to 0xfed000 by `LangDataLoadReq`; `GetMsgDataAddr(type,
  id)` = two levels of u32 offsets from 0xfed000 (read unaligned through Get4Byte / EvBinChangeAddr4).
  Bytes < 0xf0 are glyphs of font bank 0 (A = 1, a = 0x1b, space 0, full-width digits 0x35.., half-width
  0x3f..); 0xf0..0xf8 <g> bank 1..9, 0xf9 current colour, 0xfa <a> arrange (skips 3 without a window),
  0xfb page break, 0xfc <n> number (n = 0: memory card slot), 0xfd <c> colour label, 0xfe line break,
  0xff end. Banks = `fntdat[0..9]` (data 0x359a40, SPRT_DAT): FONT_TEX_PK4's 7 + FONT_EX_TEX_PK4's 3
  pages, PSMT4HH/HL 512x256, 24x24 cells 21 per row; advances in 10 tables of 210 ints (pointers
  0x32d698). Colour labels: 46 x rgb at 0x35d888, label 0x2d = the current colour (`SetCurColorRGB`, gp+0x160..
  0x162; `MsgInit` sets white). `MsgSetSubtractMode` (gp+0x163) switches ALPHA to Cd - Cs*As with inverted
  colours. Printers: `PrintMsg_ArrangeOneLine` (44 callers, Z-tested), `MsgPrintData`, `MsgPrintPage` /
  `MsgPrintPageZ` (page state at data 0x32d688 {msg, page, state 0/1/2}; `MsgPageNext` only while state
  1), `PrintNumber` / `_N` / `_One`. Coordinates centred (x -320..320, y -224..224).
- **Message viewer** (layout types 0/1, anicode.c 0x14d228..0x14e470): callers set `MsgViewSetMsg(msg0,
  msg1)` (title; body pages msg1 + 2), `MsgViewSetMode(0/1/2)`, `MsgViewSetPics(pics, n <= 8)`, then
  `LayoutAnimStart(0 or 1)`. Work `MSG_VIEW` at data 0x2f62e8, `MENU_PIC` gp-0x5a80, `CTutorial`
  gp-0x5a7c. States text (labels txt_out/gazou) and pictures (gazou_out/txt); hook lists 0x350b00 /
  0x350c20. `CTutorial` (vtable 0x341c20, 0x14): the picture beside a help page, keys 0x51..0x55 in table
  0x351190 {key, base, count}, pic = base + page + language * 0xb, drawn through a `MENU_PIC`
  (ingame/menu/menu_draw_tex.h, 0x60, two Menu_DrawTex slots cross-fading).
- **Menu window / pause menu / slot select** (anicode.c, layout types 8 / 4 / 7, MENU_WDW_PHF /
  PAUSE_PHF / SLOTSELECT_PHF): type 8 = a message (item 0x415, `MenuWdwSetMsg`) with an optional yes/no
  (`MenuWdwSetYesNo(show, yes)`; items yes_on/no_off/yes_off/no_on, cursors "R YES"/"R NO" whose
  layout positions swap with the selection); `MenuWdwGetResult` 1 yes / 2 no / 3 cancel, closes on OK /
  cancel unless `MenuWdwSetKeepOpen`; used by the story menu item/photo pages. Type 4 = pause menu:
  choices 0x41b/0x3f0/0x3f1 = messages (0x66, 0/1/2, quit 4 when title_start_flg.mode is 2), cursor item
  0x3ee placed at choice pos - choice 0 pos, colours 0x97cdd380 selected / 0x47797480; a step function
  pointer (select, quit question via layout message type 5 with (0x66, 3 or 5), pad error message).
  Type 7 = slot select: items 0x3e9/0x3ed = (0x59, 9/10), cursor 0x3ee (+10 y), `SlotSelSetSel` /
  `SlotSelReqClose`, set by 0x12dfc8 (album_load_scr.c) / 0x12fa20 (album_save_scr.c). Item positions via `B2D_PLACE`
  {handle, dx, dy} helpers 0x109420.. (`B2dPlaceInit`, `B2dPlace{Get,Set}XYByName`).
- **Message picture keys = the documents** (data 0x358a10, 0xa4 x 0xc {key, kind, pic, pic_num, menu_pic,
  msg1}; ingame/story_menu_photo.h): key = index; DocGetMsgType(key) maps kind 0..3 to msg0
  0x1d/0x1e/0x1c/0x1f; `MsgGetPicKey(msg0, msg1)` (satellite 0x2adbf0) searches it, -1 none. Owned by
  story_menu_photo.c (the document menu, phase 61); also read by 0x24f798.
- **HUD** (`ingame/menu/anim_2d.h`): `HUD_LAYOUT` 0x28 {state, file, a1, a2, arg, chr (0 Rei, 1 Miku, 2
  Kei), flash, kaku, no_camera, lens 0/1/2}; files 0x11cb..0x11e2 (table 0x351100; per-language
  0x11ce/0x11d3/0x11d8/0x11dd); items R HP, R filmicon_07.., NUM_film, NUM_flash, renz / renz1-3 (lens
  slots), R fire_4..1 graded by `CandleControl` (0x41e540, ingame/candle_ctrl.h) power +0x1c; fire wobble `HUD_FIRE` at 0x391988.
- **Common fade layer = `capCtl`** (RTTI name; `common/cmn_fade_layer.h`, `GetCmnFadeLayer`, object
  0x391080, 0x48, vtable 0x341560): per-language Bp layout `CAPTION_PHF` (0x11bd) + lang; labels are one-letter animation names "A".."Z" without
  "S" (table 0x2f2ff8), picked by `CmnFadeLayerShow/Switch/ShowFadeIn/Hide`. Alpha = base (or B2d item part
  10 when tied with `CmnFadeLayerSetItem*`) * fade / 0x80, fade a `CWrkVariable<int,0,128>`;
  `CmnFadeLayerUpdate` each frame, drawn from BpCtlMain (`CmnFadeLayerDraw`). With no label up, an idle
  label fades in during phase 0x20 while the player can act (`CmnFadeLayerIsIdleOk`; IdleShow/Hide from
  0x1a1218).
- **Pads**: driver (`system/pad/pad.c` 0x1f0138..0x1f0d78: PadOpenAll, PadReadAll, PadIsStable,
  PadEnterPressMode, the `PadSyncOne` connection state machine, steps 0/1/2/3/10/11/99 in `PAD_WRK.step`)
  fills two `PAD_WRK` of 0x200 at 0x3295c0 (`system/pad/pad.h`): port/slot +0/+4, scePadPortOpen buffer
  +0x40, held +0x140, trg +0x144, rpt +0x146 (trg, plus held every 5 frames after 10), per-button
  counters +0x14a, `connected` +0x1a4 (from scePadGetState; 0 makes IngameMain pause), `stick_dpad`
  +0x1a8 (left stick sets d-pad bits; set by options and layout anims), pressure +0x1ae, raw sticks
  +0x1be, sticks as words +0x1c2 / floats +0x1cc / bytes +0x1dc. The FF3-only game
  layer (`system/pad/pad_ctrl.h`, 0x2254b8..): `PadInitAll`, `PadFrameUpdate` (pre_super),
  `CheckPadTrg/Rpt/NowPlaySe(mask, se, pad)`, getters that return rest values while pad 0 is locked,
  per-pad locks (`IsPadLocked`, `PadLockSet/Reset`, sdata 0x373770 = gp+0x280). Button configuration:
  `pad_key[26]` at 0x32dd08 (zero in the ROM; OK +0, CANCEL +4, MENU +0x24, MAP +0x2c/+0x30, PAUSE
  +0x64) is copied each frame from `pad_key_cfg[3][26]` at 0x32dd70 by the option `OPTION_DAT.pad_cfg`
  (`PadKeyUpdate`).
- **Anime code (opcode scripts)** (`album/prg/scr_play.h`): `AnimeCodeManager` (ctor 0x129178, vtable
  0x3412f0; FF2 anicode.c's idea as a class) plays an array of u_short opcodes: bits 15..11 type (< 2: a
  frame opcode the player stops on, >= 2: a command run on the way), bits 10..9 extra-field count,
  fields via tables 0x34e248 (masks) / 0x34e268 (shifts). Each opcode goes to an `AnimeCodeInterpretor`
  (vtable 0x3415b0: Frame +8, Command +0xc, Query +0x10, CanStep +0x14). Subclasses: `...Game` (model
  motion scripts, vtables 0x341e98 / 0x341ec0, in ingame_wrk.c; models keep the manager at +0x2c and the
  interpretor at +0x28: 0x1d3a90/0x1d3ad0, frame update 0x1d3958) and `AnimeCodeInterpretorChar`
  (0x341570). Function names are still ScrPlay* / ScrHdl* / MotScr*.
  Type 0 is loop control, consumed by the cursor (`ScrCurNormalize` 0x128f38): field 0 == 2 pushes
  (opcode, passes = field 1, 0 for ever) on a 3-deep stack, field 0 == 1 jumps back or pops and calls the
  owner's Reset (vtable +0xc; the motion script's clock goes to 0 each pass); opcode 0 ends the script.
  For motion scripts a type-1 opcode starts motion field 0 and the player waits for the motion's end
  (CanStep) before `ScrPlayStep` 0x129800 moves on. The cursor is an STL-style iterator (inlined
  assignment through `ScrStkCopy` 0x2aca20; "at end" = its flag at +4). Without this player no model
  motion ever starts: the player's walk is root motion (`CharModel::GetRootMove`), so Rei only turned.
- **Phase-12 window**: callers fill `PHASE12_PARAM` (bss 0x3eb388: msg0/msg1, type 2 = message pages
  else selection, next_ok / next_alt / next_cancel phases, end callback, optional caller `CWindowMsg`) via
  `Phase12ParamSet` and request phase 12. `CWindowMsg` (RTTI name; `src/common/WindowMsg.c`,
  0x10fd70..0x110368, vtable 0x341090; phase 12's own one at sdata 0x37330c; functions still SelWin*) is
  the message/selection window on layout animation 5: mode 1 message / 2 selection / 3 confirmation
  (OK runs the callback at +0x14), result 1 OK / 2 alt / 3 cancel / 4 callback failed, auto-close at
  +0x18 (SelWinSetAutoClose).
- **CProcCtl** (RTTI; `src/common/ProcCtl.c`, 0x10fb80..0x10fd70, vtable 0x341078): every instance
  links itself into the list at sdata gp-0x7e18; `ProcCtlReleaseAll` (GameSystemEnd) calls virtual
  slot 2 `Release` on each. Subclass `CClassPtr<T>` (template code 0x2ac428..; `CClassPtr<CLightCtl>`
  vtable 0x3411a0, pointer at +8) deletes its object in Release.
- **Sound** (`src/ingame/sound/snd_ctrl.c`, FF3-only game API 0x257ae0..0x258a68 over FF2's library):
  SE banks in a 28-entry table 0x3fcf50 `{int bank; short file; short ref}`, by BD file number (header =
  file + 1; -1 = default bank 0x1a9b, loaded in Boot/LangSel/PadCheck, kept by `SndIngameStopAll(0)`).
  `SndSeBankLoad` returns 0 ready / -1 loading / -2 error and adds a reference; `SndSeBankRelease(file,
  force)` drops one; `SndSeBankReleaseAfterPlay` hands the bank to snd_util's auto release.
  `SndSePlay(id, vol, pitch, time, s3s, loop)` (id = file<<16 | sample; -3 on failure) =
  `SndBankPlay(bank, sample, 1, ...)`; `SndSeAutoPlay` = `snd_utilAutoBDPlay`. BGM: 2 slots 0x3fd030
  `{handle, file}`; `SndBgmPlay/Preload(file, vol, pitch, time, s3s, flag, slot)` queue an FF3 stream
  request (`StreamAutoPlayPitch/PreloadPitch`, prio 0xc), `SndBgmStop(time, slot)`, `SndBgmStopAll`. `SndConvVolume`
  (0..127 -> 0..0x3fff). Library at 0x269fe0..: FF2's snd_buffer.c, snd_pcmstream.c, snd_stream.c,
  stream_auto.c (0x26c8c0..0x26e0b0), snd_bank.c (0x26e0b0..0x26eb88), snd_3d.c (0x26eb88..0x26f340), snd.c
  (0x26f770..), then after cmp_eeiop.c: snd_util.c (0x2705e0..0x270a48), file_stream.c, spu_mem.c
  (0x270cf8..0x2711f0), spu_voice.c (0x2711f0..0x2712a0). stream_auto.c (done 2026-10-13): FF2's
  priority scheduler -- STREAM_QUEUE 0x60 (FF2 bitfield at +0x38; FF3 adds pitch +0x48, so file_no +0x4c ..
  play_spd +0x5c), 2 play slots bss 0x410800, wait queue in the SndInit work buffer (gp+0x564, count
  gp+0x570, max gp+0x560, disable[2] gp+0x568); BGM goes through the FF3-only `StreamAutoPlayPitch` /
  `PreloadPitch`. snd_bank.c (done): SND_BANK 0x10 (FF2's id gone) + BD/HXD file records 0x10 each in the
  work buffer (gp+0x578..0x588); no CheckHXDData. snd_3d.c (done): 30 slots 0x4108c0 (status +0x2c),
  listener 0x410e60, env data 0x333a70 (FF2 defaults), zero vector 0x333a60; FF3 split Snd3DCalc into
  DistVol/Pan/Pitch and added a never-set model hook (sdata 0x3727fc, `Snd3DSetCalcFunc` 0x26f178).
  Names corrected in snd_stream.c: 0x26c610 is SndStreamRestart, 0x26c808 SndStreamSet3D, 0x26c7e8
  SndStreamChangePlaySpeed(wrk, rate), 0x26b610 SndStreamFadeStop, 0x26b6e0 SndStreamFade, 0x26c6e0
  SndStreamIsUse; FF3 SndStreamStart takes a 5th arg (loop), SndStreamPlay a pitch. snd_buffer.c (0x269fa0..0x26ad2c,
  verify 2026-10-14): 48 SND_BUF_PLAYER 0x34 at bss 0x40e2c0 (FF2 layout: REQ_SB_PLAY payload at +0,
  s3d +0x14, play_id +0x18, use/s3d_free/pause bits +0x2a, fadestop +0x2b); id = wrk_no<<16 | play_id.
  FF3 splits Pause/Restart into slot-level Sub functions (0x26a9f0 / 0x26aa90) and adds
  SndBufAllPauseLoopSnd 0x26a148 (scene loads, save point) / SndBufAllRestartLoopSnd 0x26a1c8 (no
  caller); SndBufSet3D and SndBufferPrintStatus are gone. Wrappers: 0x26a9d0 Stop, 0x26aa70 Pause,
  0x26ab08 Restart (the matcher had called it SndBufStop). Channel-0 mute nests (`SndVolume0Mute/MuteReset`, count sdata 0x373314): the
  first mute silences, the last unmute restores. Option volumes 0..19 map to 0..256
  (`OptionGetSndVolume0/1`; which channel is BGM vs SE is open).
- **Lock table** 0x327590 (`ingame/story_menu.c`): per-id byte, bit 0 a flag (`LockTblSet/Reset`,
  `IsLockTblSet`) + signed 7-bit nesting count (`LockTblInc/Dec`, `IsLockTblCnt`); `IsLockTblOn` = either.
  Gates menus, player input, effects; known ids in story_menu.h.
- **Phase 68 Story_LoadGame_Mc** (`ingame/story_load_mc.c`): from the title (requester 29) it plays
  OUTGAME_BG_PSS behind the SAVELOAD_PHF screen (SaveLoadScr*, 0x131eb8..) with BGM_03_MENU3 and returns
  to 17 when no game was loaded (title_start_flg.load == 0); in game it fades around the outgame backdrop
  object. The memory-card load itself is McLoadGameInit/Main/End (`save_load/prg/game_data_load.c`, see below). Menu lists: MENU_LIST
  {ent*, short num, max}, entries {short id, short phase} (MenuList* 0x208e00..0x208fc8).
- **Phase 69 Story_SaveGame_Mc** (`ingame/story_save_mc.c`) is phase 68's twin: IngameSaveInit/End,
  the play time stopped meanwhile, the SAVELOAD_PHF save screen (SaveScr* 0x1336f8.., phase id 0x45),
  McSaveGameInit/Main (0x1d5b40/0x1d5c50), GameDataSaveEnd; the backdrop stays enabled when the
  requester is 110 / 115 (start-menu option / album saves). **Phase 41 Story_SaveGame**
  (`ingame/story_save.c`) is the save point menu: screen capture + OutgameBg (real world
  `ingame_wrk.b03 == 1`: layout GENJITU_SVPT_BG_PHF hiding item 0x3f0; else MenuBg MENUBG_PHF label
  0x3fb), SavePointMenu* (`ingame/savepoint/savepoint_menu.c`, 0x135690..0x135e08: SAVEPOINT_PHF + language,
  MenuCsr 0x29, BP_OBJ cursor 0x3ee rows 30 px apart, rows MENU_PHASE_ROW {item, msg, help, phase} at ROM
  0x34f160: save -> 0x45, -> 8, leave -> -1; message kind 0x69; yes/no window = LayoutAnim type 6 showing
  the cursor row's help line, question (0x66, 3) starting on no; cancel jumps to and picks the last row;
  the cursor row is kept between visits unless GPHASE_CTRL.from is 0x20); row phase
  5 asks yes/no, the chosen phase runs as a linked child, back from it GPhaseTreeReset and the menu
  again. StorySaveGameReq 0x250850 (from 0x1a1cb8) requests 41.
- **Phase 40 Story_LoadGame** shows the **game over menu** (`ingame/gameover_menu.c`, 0x133960..0x1340f8,
  GameOverMenu*, called by one_Story_LoadGame 0x1d7f68): the save point menu's twin over GAMEOVER_PHF,
  MenuCsr 0x28, message kind 0x6c, rows ROM 0x34eea8 {0x41b -> 8, 0x3f0 -> 5 Title_Mode (asks)}, cursor rows
  40 px apart (+10), row text +10 px per row; the row is reset when GPHASE_CTRL.from is 10.
- **Outgame backdrop** (`outgame/outgame_bg.c`, sdata 0x372088..0x3720b4): OutgameBgStart(type, file,
  arg): 0 TITLE_OBJ movie (fades 0x80/10 per frame via TitleObjFadeIn/Out), 1 BP_OBJ layout (arg: an
  item hidden), 2 MenuBg; a cover BP_OBJ of OUTGAME_TTLBG_PHF plays "in" while OutgameBgEnable is set
  and "out" after OutgameBgDisable. **BP_OBJ** (0x18, `system/b2d/BpObj.c`, 0x136df8..0x1374d8):
  {file, state 0 new / 1 loading / 2 loaded / 3 in / 4 shown / 5 out, played (BpObjPlayOnce), ScrollBar*
  (a B2d_ScrollBar3, BpObjAdd/Del/DrawScrollBar), cursor list (BP_OBJ_CSR {next, id, B2D_GRID_SPR*} from
  the system heap, BpObjAddCsr / SetCsrPos / SetCsrDisp / FreeCsr), +0x14}; used by OutgameBg, the save /
  load screens, the save point menu, the album screens, menu_cassette, alg_manage, the yes/no menu
  0x1ed7f8. **B2D_GRID_SPR** (`system/b2d/B2dGridSpr.c`, 0x130638..0x130950, 0xc): {item, x, y, dx, dy}
  shorts; origin from the item's parts 6/7 (frozen), SetPos(col, row) = origin + step * (col, row);
  the menus' cursors. **ScrollBar** (`system/b2d/ScrollBar.c`, 0x135e28..0x136df8 + inline virtuals
  0x2acae0..0x2acfb8): RTTI ScrollBar (0x10, vtable 0x341378) {rows, page, cursor, first shorts at +4..+0xa,
  track length +0xc}; slots +0 pos rate first/rows, +4 page rate page/rows (both clamped 0..1), +8/+0xc
  dtors, +0x10 SetRange(rows, page), +0x14 SetPos(cursor, first), +0x18 SetLength, +0x1c CanScrollUp
  (first > 0), +0x20 CanScrollDown (first < rows - page), +0x24 IsScrollable, +0x28 knob pos, +0x2c knob
  len (length * rate). B2d_ScrollBar3 (0x24, vtable 0x341340): three knob items {top, mid, bottom} at
  +0x10, track origin x/y +0x1c/+0x20 from a frame item (h = length, parts 6/7 = origin); Draw 0x136720
  stacks the pieces via B2dScrollBarDrawKnob3 0x2acd28 (middle stretched to len - ends, rgb 0x80 / dim 0x50).
  B2d_ScrollBar1 (0x1c, vtable 0x341308): one knob item, Draw 0x136c98 stretches it. Constructors take
  item ids or names (two emitted copies each) and freeze the knobs' parts 6, 7, 8, 9, 17. The arrows
  beside a bar: MenuScrollArrowDisp 0x2090a8 (menu_bg.c; per direction arrow alpha 0x80 animated /
  0x40 frozen, effect item shown only when it can scroll).
- **Memory-card load screen** (`outgame/save_load_scr.c`, 0x130950..0x132118; phase 68): SAVELOAD_PHF
  + language, five slots 0x6e px apart. Reads McLoadGame's work **MC_LOAD_WRK** 0x3282e0 (type in
  save_load/prg/game_data_load.h) {msg (yes/no message 0x83, msg), end_timer +4, mode +8 (0 new, 1, 2
  steps run, 3 done), step +9 (MLG_STEP_*; 5 / 12 / 13 slots hidden, 6 select, 7 ask), slot, yesno,
  msg_timer +0xc, slot_flg[5] +0x10 (1 save, -1 bad data), map snapshot TIM2s[5] +0x18, their file
  numbers +0x30, chapter title TIM2s[5] +0x48, their file numbers +0x60}. Per slot a fade (bss
  0x390c78, +0x10 a frame); item hooks print the cursor slot's info through `outgame/save_info_disp.c`
  (0x1340f8..0x134f60: thumbnail to TBP 0x2cc4 / CBP 0x2bc0 drawn 108x60 at 100/108 width, play time,
  difficulty (0x69, 0xc+d), chapter (0x7a, n), player (0x69, 0x10+p); album twins for the album
  screens). The **play data head** (5 x 0x24 at 0x3f5df0, getters 0x202b68..0x202c88 in mc_set_data.c,
  filled at save by 0x202a80): PLAY_TIME, clear count +0x10, chapter +0x14 (ingame_wrk +0x28), map area
  +0x18, signed bytes +0x1c (ingame_wrk +4), +0x1d (+2), +0x1e player, +0x1f difficulty, +0x20 finder
  flag, +0x21 data flag.
- **Memory-card load steps** (`save_load/prg/game_data_load.c`, FF3-only, 0x1d4678..0x1d5b40): the
  load twin of game_data_save.c (same step shapes as FF2's GameDataSaveMc*). McLoadGameMain: mode 0
  clears title_start_flg.load, 1, 2 runs McLoadGameMcMain (15 steps, jump table 0x359f50; each even
  init step falls into its wait step in the same frame): 0/1 dir check, 2/3 card file 1 (play data
  heads) -> slot_flg, 4/5 per save the map snapshot (RS101_R01_TM2 0x133f + MapSnapGetNo(area,
  chapter) 0x1f6e70, finder saves CLEARDATA_TM2) and chapter title (0x840 + B02, at least
  SAVELOAD_CHAPTER01_TM2; finder SAVELOAD_CHAPTER15_TM2) by FileLoadReqEE, 6 select (left/right over
  saved slots, cancel ends), 7 yes/no, 8/9 card file 0 (system), 10/11 card file slot+2 (the game,
  then EventAfterLoad 0x19b9e8), 12/13 error message (75/90-frame guard; a new card restarts at 0),
  14 "loaded" (OK/cancel or 150 frames -> title_start_flg.load = 1). Messages (0x83, n): 1 no card,
  2 card error, 0xe ask, 0xf load error, 0x10 loaded, 0x18 no data, 0x19 damaged, 0x1d / 0x20
  loading, 0x21 no save, 0x22 select, 0x33 / 0x34 system / game file damaged. Card file buffer sdata
  gp-0x2a18.
- **Memory-card save** (`save_load/prg/game_data_save.c`, 0x1d5b40..0x1d7a58; phase 69): McSaveGameInit /
  Main / GameDataSaveEnd over GameDataSaveMcMain's 35 steps (MGS_STEP_* in game_data_save.h): FF2's 31
  plus 8/9 (the chosen slot fades out, SaveScrIsSlotFadedOut, then GameDataSaveMcSnapFree) and 16/17
  (the written slot's textures reload, GameDataSaveMcSnapLoadSlot 0x1d6358) before 18 "saved"
  (OK/cancel -> **title_start_flg.save** 0x3275b4 = 1, read by the phase code 0x1aa788 / 0x1a5c58).
  10/11 merge the card's system file (TitleMenuItemOpenBackup / Develop / Merge) before 12/13 write
  three files in the order of the .data table 0x3283d8 {0 system, slot + 2, 1 heads} (index 1 patched
  per save; the new-directory save uses 0x3283e8, slot 0). Each write copies between EventEnd 0x19ba08
  and EventAfterSave 0x19ba28. Card states: damaged directory + room -> 19 remake (dir delete, new
  make), no directory + room -> 23 new make, unformatted -> 29 format (60 frames "formatted", then new
  make), no room -> message 0x17. MC_SAVE_WRK: fmt_end_timer +4, save_cnt +0xd, msg_timer +0x10.
- **Memory-card save screen** (`outgame/save_scr.c`, 0x132118..0x133960; phase 69): the load screen's
  code over McSaveGame's work **MC_SAVE_WRK** 0x328358 (type in save_load/prg/game_data_save.h, variable
  in game_data_save.c) {msg +0 (0xb overwrite?, 0x1f new file), mode +8, step +9, slot +0xa, slot_num
  +0xb (saves + 1, max 5; set by GameDataSaveMcSaveFileSelPad 0x1d6760, the cursor wraps over it),
  yesno +0xc, slot_flg[5] +0x14, TIM2s[5] +0x20, int[5] at +0x38 (>= 0), +0x50 (!= 0), +0x68 (>= 0)}.
  Same items and ROM tables (0x34f058.., hook list 0x34eda8). Differences: steps 5/14/15 hide the
  slots and 17 the chosen one; the fades hold in 5/14/15/17 and in step 9 the chosen slot fades out
  (McSaveGame 0x1d69f0 waits on SaveScrIsSlotFadedOut 0x132388, then writes, step 10); the yes/no
  choices show in steps 7/19/20/23/24/29/30; the arrows only when slot_num >= 2; the card-slot text
  is not faded; the hidden title is the load one (0x3ec, R ttl_load2). SaveScrMsgIsOpen 0x1336b8 gates
  McSaveGame's pad steps (7 callers).
- **Event result queue** (`ingame/event/ev_queue.c`): EV_QUEUE_WRK 0x3f7c90 {kind, idx, anim, msg_anim,
  ent*, step fn, s_char order[4][50] (-1 free, 0.. queued, -2 done)}; ROM lists per kind at 0x332ab0
  (0x3622c0 / 0x362460 / 0x3625d0 / 0x3626d8; EV_QUEUE_ENT 0x1a: id, msg0, msg1, item-get picture,
  msg_pic_key_dat document key, msg2, model + 3 states (ManmdlAdvanceState), finder flag
  (FinderCamFlagSet, 0x325198), item (ItemUse), map area side). Event command 0x1a0d78 adds,
  0x1a0da0 starts and polls EvQueueMain until -1; 0x221360 / 0x221820 use kind 1.

## Core systems and entry points

- **Story structure** (EVENT_OBJ, decoded 2026-10-19 with `evdump.py`): an event tree of 0x6f5 events.
  Root 0's sub-events are 1 (shared events: the manor bed 2 and the house beds 3 / 6 that switch worlds,
  5 the house setup that auto-runs at every game start -- the cat (NPC 2) and Miku active, wake / sleep
  movies 27 / 47, random ghosts off in areas 47..58 --, 7 the house events EVENT_REBOOT 7 reopens),
  88 (the story: 89, then 15 chapter events opened by EV_NOW_CHAPTER on ingame_wrk.b01 in the order
  148 (0, the prologue), 321 (1), 437 (2), 90 (3), 183 (4), 244 (5), 488 (6), 565 (7), 646 (8), 707 (9),
  747 (10), 820 (11), 896 (12), 960 (13), 1032 (14); each one's end sets the next chapter), 1060 (the
  normal start: sound loads, a ghost, visible effects), 1602, 1746 (the developers' debug start), 1754,
  1763. Each chapter event's first sub-event is a **chapter-select entry** (149, 322, 438, 91, 184, 245,
  489, 566, 647, 708, 748, 821, 897, 961, 1033): no open condition, sets the chapter and marks the
  others ended, so normal play (149 runs first) never reaches them and a game that starts with b01 = N
  runs N's; 438 / 91 / 489 / 708 / 961 move Rei into the house (GO_REAL_WORLD, EVENT_REBOOT 7,
  AREA_CHANGE 47, PLYR_POS_SET (39, 39), the wake-up motion, FADE_IN). CHAPTER_SEL_INIT returns 0 in
  retail. The parts after it are split by world (CHECK_GAME_WORLD 0 dream / 1 house) and chained by
  EV_STATE [5, previous]. **EVENT_DB_OBJ** (EventSetMode 1 / 2) is EVENT_OBJ with one condition moved:
  1060 never opens and 1746 does (fade in, PLYR_PAD 1, PHOTO_UNLOCK, items, random ghosts on); mode 2
  also keeps the start area through Story_NowLoading (IngameNowLoadingMain), FF3's leftover debug
  start. Areas 47..52 are the house (map_menu.ecb rooms 1..6: 47 Rei's bedroom with the bed, 48 the
  living room; the cat's labels are in 48..50), 1..46 the manor (rooms 101..509), 53..59 have no map
  room. `reipan/debug/reipan_warp.cpp` starts games in any of this (open-items.md "Starting elsewhere").
- In-game 2D map position (`ingame/map/MapSnap.c`, 0x1f5ba0..0x1f73b8, 2026-10-15): MapSnapInit
  (IngameInit) makes CMarkDat (8 floors x 5 layers of maps_*.b2d, table 0x34c400) and loads
  MAP_MENU_ECB (room rows, 0xc bytes: room, sub, group, area, place, snap_no, flags, world; save block
  53) and MAP_MENU_STAT_ECB (layer transforms: world = (layout + ofs) * scale, 4 / -4). MapSnapUpdate
  (IngameDrawSub, every frame) finds the layout item under the player (item names "Rs<room>..r<sub>")
  and makes its row current: map_bgm's music, ingame_wrk._28 (the save header's place), visited bits
  0x13 with MAP_UPDATE. MapSnapGetNo gives the save screens' room picture (+ RS101_R01_TM2).
- Map menu floor layouts (`ingame/map/MarkDat.c` 0x10e650..0x10f150 and `ingame/map/MapRec.c`
  0x10e230..0x10e650, 2026-10-15; FF3-only C++ class files): table 0x34c400 (data, writable) holds
  the maps_*_b2d (2ddata/rec) file per floor and layer, 8 x 5: floors 0..4 B2F..3F, 5..7 world 1's
  1F..3F (the *_KSW files); each file + 1 is its *_PHF. MarkDatLoadAll (one_Boot_Load) puts the 23
  present files into Bp entries at EE 0xfcf000.. (16-aligned, BP_CTL_FLAG_PAUSE) for the whole game;
  missing files become -1 in the table. CMarkDat (0x46c bytes) maps floors through ingame_wrk.b03
  (world 1: +3), plays 'NewLabel_0' per layer and measures its world rect (MapSnapLayerGetRect).
  MarkDatMeasure (MapSnapMarkDatDraw) places/scales each floor relative to floor 2 layer 0's map
  origin and measures the screen rect via CMapRec: a static (0x379a10) int rect grown by B2d
  sprite/quad hooks that draw nothing (MapRecMeasure: BpCtlDrawOne with the hooks, then BpCtlSetHooks).
  CMapRec is 0x28 bytes: +0x18 is the map window (x, y, w, h) that the map draw 0x1f87b0 sets and the
  mark draw / map sprite hook clip against.
- Map screen marks (`ingame/map/MarkDoor.c` 0x10f150..0x10f820, `ingame/map/MarkEvent.c`
  ..0x10fb80, 2026-10-15): the map screen object (bss 0x3f3360, ctors 0x1f8118/0x1f8168 in
  m_plyr_camera.c) holds a CMarkDoor at +0x90 (80 records of 0x60 bytes: sprite or quad hook words,
  texture pair, a door mark id whose doors -- door ECB rows with short +0xe == id, DoorGetMarkDoors
  0x154720 -- colour it light blue 0x50e69680 when one has state bit 3 and none is locked) and a
  memberless CMarkEvent at +0x1e98. The map sprite hook 0x1f7978 records door items there
  (MarkDoorAddSprite), hides items whose SET_2DMAP_DRAW flag is 0 (MarkEventIsDrawn: table 0x2efae8
  by floor (+3 in world 1), layer, item id) and swaps two layer-4 items in area 0x2a for a bobbing
  sprite (MarkEventSpecialSprite); 0x1f87b0 then draws the marks (MarkDoorDraw).
- Room effects and point-light labels (`ingame/map/MapEff.c`, 0x1f4788..0x1f5ba0): 128 effect rows
  (bss 0x3ef3b0) made from FurnSave kind-0 rows when a room is shown (types: 0..3 candles, 4 lamp,
  100/101 deform, 200/201, 300..303, 400..403, 500..502, 600), shown/hidden with their furniture row
  (SET_EFF_VISIBLE); 64 point-light label rows (bss 0x3f13b0, save block 52; SET_EVENT_REC_HIT); the
  seven room load slots (bss 0x3f16b0).

- Memory-card save data (`system/mc/prg/mc_set_data.c` + `system/mc/dat/save_data.c`, 2026-10-14): a
  card file is its manifest's blocks back to back plus a 4-byte signed byte-sum checksum (mc_cmn.c
  0x200b30/0x200b60). A manifest entry is `int cb(void **addr)`: it stores the block address and
  returns the size (FF2's filled an MC_SAVE_DATA). Game slot = 60 blocks (ingame_wrk 0x4c, the finder
  works, photo mode, events, enemies, puzzles, map hit 0x2600, ...), system file = one 0x10 block
  (0x3eb368, FUN_001cfeb0 in ingame_wrk.c), album header = 0x10 per side (0x3905b0), album data =
  info 0x20c (0x3905d0) + photo pixels 0xe8000 (pointer table 0x372c08). Play-data head per slot
  (MC_PLAY_DATA_HEAD, 0x24): play time, finder counters summed (FUN_001a5e60), ingame_wrk._28
  (chapter), map area, ingame_wrk.b04/b02/b00, difficulty, finder state +0xc, data flag. Directory
  sizes: icon.sys 0x3c4 + files + icon model (ZERO3GAME_ICO / ZERO3ALBUM0_ICO) + 5 (game) or 2 (album)
  clusters + 2.
- Maths (`common/vu0math.c`, 0x1ef370..0x1efcf0, FF3-only, reconstructed 2026-10-14): FF3 replaces
  libm's trig with VU0 macro-mode polynomials. `NormalizeAngle` 0x1ef5e8 = x - trunc(x/2pi)*2pi (sdata
  quadword 0x35b630 {0, 1/2pi, -2pi, pi/180}); `Vu0Sinf` 0x1ef758 folds into [-pi/2, pi/2] and runs the
  Taylor series to x^9 (0x35b610), clamped to [-1, 1]; `Vu0Cosf` = Vu0Sinf(x + pi/2); `Vu0Atanf`
  0x1ef620 = pi/4 + odd series in (x-1)/(x+1) (`Vu0AtanPoly` 0x1ef578, table data 0x329550 whose x slot
  Vu0Atanf overwrites); `g3dAtan2f` 0x1ef6b0 on Vu0Atanf; `Vu0Acosf` 0x1ef860 = g3dAtan2f(sqrt(1-x^2), x).
  `RotVectorX/Y/Z(rot, dst, src)` rotate the unit matrix at data 0x3293c0 and apply it;
  `RotVectorZXY(out, in, rot)` passes `in` to every step (only the last non-zero angle survives unless
  out == in). `GetTrgtRot` 0x1efc28 (FF2 unit_ctl.c, rot no longer cleared; FF2's clearing form is
  `UnitGetTrgtRot` 0x25e9e0 in ingame/plyr/unit_ctl.c with GetTrgtRotY / RotRngChk / OutSightChk /
  ConvertRot2Dir, GetMidPos, GetLength2D, IsPosInView and the stick readers GetStickRaw(mode) /
  GetStickRot (10.0 = centred) / GetStickDir8 / GetDirTrg), `RotLimitChk`, `GetRandValF`
  (/2147483520), `IntLogBase` 0x1ef8f8 (texture TW/TH), and dead `LogBasef`, `HermiteVector`,
  `BSplineVector`. 0x294800 is libm `logf` (the matcher had atan2f).
- In-game frame: `IngameMain(skip_flags)` 0x1d18c8 (mission, PlyrCheckAction, EventMain, PlayerMain,
  EffectSlotMain, enemies, CameraMain; opens menu 37 / map 58 / pause 44 from `pad_key`) and
  `IngameDrawSub` 0x1d1ed0; their skip-mask bits are documented above each function in
  `src/ingame/ingame.c`, the frame's flags are kept at sdata 0x370abc (`IngameGetDrawFlags`). Part of
  IngameMain's map draw runs from a GS callback at 0x1d1c38 (no Ghidra function; it sits inside
  IngameMain's body). `IngameSetDrawEnable` computes draw-enable words (sdata 0x371300/04/08/48,
  0x37126c/7c) from the map camera (`MapCamGetCurData` 0x3f3260) and the leaf phase; IngameDrawSub
  resets them to 1. Sound listener: `IngameSndListenerSet` -> `SndSetListener` 0x26ec78 from the camera
  or the player (up = {0,-1,0,0}). `IngameInit` 0x1d0ed8 / `IngameEnd` 0x1d1730 set up and tear down
  ~60 subsystems; `IngameNowLoadingMain` 0x1d12d0 is one_Story_NowLoading's load/start steps.
- Miku's slow mode (`ingame/plyr/plyr_gauge.c` 0x129968..0x12a068, written 2026-10-14, verify): a `PLYR_GAUGE` 0xc
  {disp 0..1, fill 0..1000, loop SE id} in the photo mode work +0x3c0 (GetAlbumSaveDisp). Player 1 only.
  PlyrDamage fills it (dmg * 0.07 * PlyrEquip3Rate); with >= 50 her camera button (PlyrActionCtrl) spends
  50 and sets player flag 0x100000 (SE 0x1b7f0006, loop 0x1b7f0007, FUN_001978a8(1)); it drains
  GetFrameTimeScale * PlyrEquip4Rate a frame and ends at 0 or on the button (SE 0x1b7f0008). While on,
  PlyrGaugeTimeStep (the player's timer step, 19 callers) is x0.67 and PlyrGaugeDmgRate (hold damage) 0.34.
  PlyrGaugeTimeStep also returns the holding enemy's ENE_WRK +0x560 (step) in hold reactions
  (modes 1..4, dmg_type 3..6/10/11). HUD bar: HudSlowGaugeDraw 0x1be2a0 (finder_view_hud.c), colour rows
  0 / 1 on / 2 under 50 at 0x359790.
- Players: three playable characters (Rei, Miku, Kei); `PLYR_WRK` (0x350, `ingame/plyr/player.h`) x3 at
  data 0x32f4a0, `GetPlyrWrk(i)`, current player work pointer 0x32758c, current player number
  INGAME_WRK +0. `PlayerMain` 0x231928 (player.c, reconstructed 2026-10-14): skipped when GPHASE_CTRL.next is
  0x22, under lock 2 (unless mode 7) or without a model (+0x2e4); runs PlyrCtrlOptCheck, PlyrAnimeEndCheck,
  PlyrEneCheck (PlyrBattleCheck / NearEneInfo / PlyrEneTouchCheck / PlyrVibCheck), PlyrStatusCtrl,
  PlyrMapObjFlagCtrl 0x23fef8, PlyrMapCamCheck, then one handler: hold flags 0x1c00000 (0x23b4f8 /
  PlyrFinderCtrl 0x232590 / 0x23b898), crawl flags 0x1c000000 (PlyrCrawlStart 0x240448 / PlyrCrawlCtrl
  0x240680 / PlyrCrawlEnd 0x240538), else by PLYR_WRK.mode +0x144 (0 PlyrNormalCtrl 0x232478 (mode 11
  PlyrMode11Ctrl first), 1..4 PlyrDamageCtrl 0x232c00, 5 PlyrMode5Ctrl 0x23e788 (the ladder),
  6 PlyrDoorCtrl 0x236550, 7 PlyrDeathCtrl 0x231d30 (sticky, PlyrSetMode; PlyrDeathStart 0x231c70 enters
  it), 8 PlyrMode8Ctrl 0x2372d8 (flash recoil), 10 PlyrMode10Ctrl 0x236990 (player 2's stance)), then
  PlyrActionCtrl 0x237570 (the action button per character: 0 flash, 1 camera PlyrGaugeSlowStart/End, 2 mode 10
  start/end, which turns pad_wrk[0].stick_dpad (data 0x329768) on/off) and PlyrAnimeCtrl 0x239158 (four
  choosers fill one anime byte from 0xff: event poses by flags2 0x3f800 (table 0x360c08), moves by flags2
  0x3ff (table 0x360fa8, start / continuing), stop anime by the model's walk step, stance / low-HP anime 4 /
  idle variants after idle_time with flag 0x80000; then +0x192 = walk step). player.c is pinned to
  0x231628..0x2394a8 (2026-10-14; a file end at 0x236920/0x236940 splits it in two, open-items.md).
  Direction objects (map hits of type 0 / 1 with a facing at +0x24: PlyrMapDirAnimeCtrl 0x240938 sets
  flags2 0x10 along / 0x20 against / 0x200 across and the side-walk state 0x40/0x80/0x100)
  takes its anime from PlyrDirAnimeNo 0x2382c0: table 0x360c98 (mask, anime, anime from idle 0) picks
  a side-step start 0x26/0x29/0x2c/0x2f/0x32/0x35/0x38/0x3b, start +1 is its loop, the loop goes to a
  stop (0x28/0x2b/0x2e/0x31/0x34/0x37/0x3a/0x3d) by the model's walk step when the model's anime word 0
  (FUN_001d3b20) says the loop point is reached. The right stick turns the head (PlyrLookCtrl 0x2359c8,
  from the player model's frame 0x146cb8): PLYR_WRK +0x320 pitch -20..40 deg, +0x324 yaw +-30 deg.
  Doors: PlyrDoorStart 0x236398(door id, side) reads the door record (DOOR_DAT, 0xb0 bytes at data
  0x329a20.., DoorDatGet 0x1f4450) +0x10 + side*4 for the player anime. Equipment levels sbss 0x373820
  (7 bytes; max per slot sdata 0x371b88 {1,3,1,3,0,3,1}; PlyrEquipObtain / PlyrEquipLvUp; equipment 3 / 4
  give rates 0x360070 / 0x360080); CExtraMissionShelt::CarryStore keeps each work's equip[3] on the system heap
  over the story game's reload after an extra mission (PlyrEquipBackup / PlyrEquipRestore).
  Player models (plyr_sub.c 0x23eca8..): PlyrMdlCreate(no, kind, mot) builds a CharPlayerModel
  (operator new 0x1c0, ctor 0x145c50, load FUN_00145090(chr, type, model pk4, motion pk4, shape)) from
  the per-character tables: PLYR_MDL_DAT data 0x361448 {chr, type, SE bank SYS_REI/MIKU/KEI_BD_BD},
  model rows data 0x330ae0 -> 0x361460 / 0x3614d8 / 0x361528 ({model pk4, shape}; row = PLYR_WRK
  +0x2e9 costume, kind 0 / 1 / 2 + costume_sub +0x2ea; row sizes n0 + n1 + n2 from sdata 0x371c80..90:
  Rei 1+1+4, Miku and Kei 1+1+2), motion data 0x361578 [chr][mot] (Rei CH000_REAL / CH000_LIGHT;
  mot 1 sets flags 0x8000), plus AcsModels (0x28, vtable 0x3412a0, file A000_PK4 + n): A001 / A002 on
  joint 0x13, A000 on joint 7, the accessory +0x2eb (A000 + {9,13,17}[chr] + acs - 1) on joint 2
  (PlyrMdlSetup 0x23f0f8 after the load). Joints: CharPlayerModel vtable +0x34 (0x145528) maps a joint
  to its SGDCOORDINATE index, +0x2c (0x1d2e90) transforms a point (PlyrGetJointPos 0x23fc78; joint 0x1b's
  angle about y is the facing, PlyrGetJointRotY 0x23fbc8). PLYR_WRK +0x2e0 is a 0x14-byte fade envelope
  (PLYR_MDL_FX: sine rise / hold / linear fall, or a pulse) handed to the model each frame (FUN_001d3110).
  Ladder (mode 5, PlyrLadderStartBottom 0x23e208 / Top 0x23e330, LADDER_DAT 0x90 from the list
  FUN_001991a8(FUN_00199268(), id)): anime 0x5f / 0x62 get on, 0x5e rest, 0x60 / 0x63 a step up / down
  counting PLYR_WRK +0x340 against +0x341 = (height - 6) / 2, 0x61 / 0x64 get off; two cameras by step.
  Crawl (flags 0x4000000 down with anime 0x9e, 0x8000000 crawling, 0x10000000 up; loop SE 0x1b7f0005
  from Miku's bank in +0x34c, rustle 0x1c4f every 100 units): PlyrCrawlMove turns by the stick x beyond
  100 and moves by y beyond 90 (pad_key[2] faster, pad_key[5] turns faster).
  Moving (plyr_sub.c 0x240dd0..0x2430e0): the frame's move is the model's motion root move
  (PlyrMotMoveGet 0x2430e0: FUN_001452d0(mdl, axis, anime_attr), x/y negated, into PLYR_WRK.move +0x1c0;
  crawling x0.6), turned by the facing and added to pos in PlyrMoveUpdate 0x242080 (PlayerMain), which
  also adds the turns PLYR_WRK.turn +0x1e0 (times the timer step) and turn_cb +0x1d0 to rot; +0x30 / +0x40
  keep the last two moves. flags2 bits 0..3 are the speed (PlyrMoveSpeedCtrl 0x2422c8: 1 run =
  pad_key[2], 2 walk = the stick push); 0x400 a quick turn (PlyrQuickTurnCtrl 0x242c30, tank control),
  0x800 / 0x1000 / 0x2000 a turn standing / walking / running, 0x4000 > 60 deg, 0x8000 > 135 deg,
  0x10000 / 0x20000 its side (PlyrTurnStart 0x242590; event turns copy rot to +0xa0 / +0xc0 with the
  target in +0xc4); 0x4000000 the tank control's side step. Option b11 picks the control: 0 camera-
  relative (PlyrMoveDirCtrl 0x240dd0), 1 tank (PlyrTankMoveCtrl 0x241128: d-pad 1..3 / 5..7 turn
  4.2 deg per frame, 4 turns to the back). The viewfinder (PlyrFinderMoveCtrl 0x2413a8): the right stick
  (TitleMenuMovePhase(5)) turns and pitches (PLYR_WRK +0x238, -60..60 deg; held: within +0x244 / +0x248)
  and moves finder_x/y (PlyrFinderPointMove 0x242d80: 302..338 x 209..239, back to 320, 224); the
  stick FUN_0025f410(6) steps (anime 0x19..0x1c). Drawing (PlyrMdlDraw 0x23f548(plyr, pass)): body by
  vtable +0x20 (0x146038) or FUN_001d2d98, AcsModel 1 (2 in photo mode) during the camera / flash anime,
  A000 with the light motion (flags 0x8000), the accessory before (acs 2) or after the body; AcsModel
  alpha fades run from PLYR_WRK.req280[no] (PlyrAcsFadeCtrl 0x23f338; AcsModel +0x20 speed, +0x24 alpha
  0..128). PlyrMdlFxGhostCtrl 0x243798 pulses the model while ghost model id 0x1a is within 60.
  Death (PlyrDeathCtrl, PLYR_WRK.death_step +0x233, counter sdata 0x3719ec): steps 1..3 held (approach
  camera on the midpoint, white fade), 4..10 a fatal hit (anime 0x99, damage camera, the ghost follows
  hold_move, FUN_00197e28(3) / album-view 0x13ac88.. then enemies released), 0xb..0xd the fall (anime 0x8f),
  0xe: lock 0x18 -> revival at full HP (0x10 sets PLYR_FLG_REVIVED 0x2000000, PlyrDeathIsRevived) else game
  over (FUN_0019c380(1), FUN_001d7a58, 0xf). The effect work's disp_flg (FF2 eff_wrk, data 0x2f6a10) is
  off while the death camera runs (lock 0xb).
  Viewfinder: PlyrFinderEneCheck 0x2342f0 rebuilds each enemy slot's flags 0x40/0x80/0x200/0x400 (keep mask
  sdata 0x371a68); FrameInsideChk 0x234f78 projects ENE_WRK.tpos (+0x50c, a pointer) through
  0x180710 (FF2 GetCamI2DPos) and tests the 520x328 frame (sdata 0x371ac0) around PLYR_WRK.finder_x/y.
  PLYR_WRK.rot +0x50 (rot[1] the facing), +0x140 the MhCtl hit object, +0x33c mode 10 timer.
  Damage and holds (PlyrDamageCtrl, by PLYR_WRK.dmg_type +0x114): 1/7/8/9 a hit (damage at once, mode 4),
  6 a timed hit (the enemy's ENE_WRK.hit_dmg +0x510), 3/4/5/10/11 a ghost's hold: mode 1 rolls two
  escape windows (PLYR_WRK +0x198..+0x1ac; PlyrHoldEscapeCheck 0x235728 counts PAD_KEY_06/07 and flash
  presses in them), mode 2 waits for them and the grab anime, then breaks free (item 8, lens function 2 or a
  flash press: PlyrFlashRelease 0x236fa0 / PlyrHoldShakeOff 0x237130) or starts the hold (mode 3: damage
  every dmg_time +0x14c, ENE_DAT.atk (+0x9e of ENE_WRK.dat +0x1e8) x FUN_00129fd0's rate; mashing
  wears ENE_WRK.hold_pow +0x4d4 down; the enemy moves by PLYR_WRK.hold_move +0x220 each frame).
  Hold anime by attacker kind PLYR_WRK._147: data 0x32fe90 [3][16] (grab, hold, let go).
  Flash: equipment levels sbss gp+0x330 (7 bytes, PlyrGetEquipLv 0x237840; [0] Rei can flash, [1] the
  count level -> {4, 8, 12, 16} sdata 0x371b90), PLYR_WRK.flash_num +0x268 (int; the HUD reads the low
  half), flash wait float gp+0x32c (PlyrFlashIsReady). SetPlyrAnime 0x238020 (a0 anime, a1 blend, a2
  phase, a3 attr, t0 arg, f12 start; 0xff = from the tables PlyrAnimeBlendFrames 0x237898 /
  PlyrAnimeStartPhase 0x237d58 / PlyrAnimeAttr 0x237ec0) calls the player model's vtable +0x40
  (CharPlayerModel 0x1450e0) with f13 = 1.0; +0x60 (0x146640) is the walk step 0..3.
  HP per player and difficulty: table 0x3613b8 [3][4] (PlyrMdlGetHpMax 0x239a58, plyr_param.c). Rumble lives in player.c in FF3: VibrateRequest1/2 0x235d10/0x235d68 write
  pad_wrk[].vib_* when OPTION_DAT.vibration (+4) is on; PlyrVibCheck's heartbeat band is 12..120 (FF2
  300..3000: FF3 distances are 1/25 of FF2's). `roku_pzl.c`'s range (0x23e788..0x243798) is player
