  model/control code PlayerMain calls every frame, not a puzzle (name is a link-order guess). The "player-camera" works are the two
  companion NPCs (event NPC 0 / 1; `ingame/photo/n_plyr_camera.c` 0x21e360..0x222170, FF3-only despite
  FF2's file name): `PLYR_CAMERA_WRK` 2 x 0x150 at 0x3f62c0 behind a 0x10-byte head 0x3f62b0 (serif /
  facial table bytes; head + works = save block 54, PlyrCameraSaveGet). A work: +0 the 0x48-byte act
  block `PLYR_CAMERA_ACT` (the NPC's AI: +0x1e NPC number, +0x1f position label, +0x26 step; cleared
  0x21a678, reset 0x21a818, stepped by 0x21a890 while +0xf0 free != 0; hooks idle 0x220510 / 0x220558,
  watch 0x2209e8 / 0x220a28, chat 0x220270, label 0x220100, and condition / init / step triples
  greet 0x220bf8 / 0x220c48 / 0x220ca0 (+0x3c 0 -> 1 in range -> 2 done), report 0x2212b0 / 0x221308 /
  0x221360 and report1 0x221780 / 0x2217e8 / 0x221820 (play EvQueue kinds 0 / 1, NPC 0 only:
  EvQueueGetNext >= 0), chat3 0x221a50.. (once, +0x38), chat4 0x221de8.., greet motion 0x222080 /
  0x222088; the steps share +0x2a fade count, +0x2b fade timer, +0x2c step, +0x30 motion-wait flag;
  called from the act scripts of `ingame/npc/npc_act.c`, e.g. op 0x42 (0x21baf0) = greet init then step until 1),
  +0x48 three facial channels (cur / req / state; channel 0 holds, 1 replays, 2 plays once, driven
  through MDL_MOT vtable +0x10 / +0x2c / +0x3c), +0x5c the CharNpcModel, +0x70 root move, +0x80 turn
  (from the motion callback, under pi/6 ignored), +0x90 pos, +0xa0 rot, +0xb0 area, +0xb4 motion,
  +0xc0 eye point, +0xd4 eye on, +0xdc model type (0 real, 1 dream side, 2.. costumes; NPC 0's types 3 / 4
  link cat 0 via FUN_00142848(0, 2 / 4, on)), +0xe0 ready, +0xe4 visible (area is a drawn room), +0xe8
  active (NPC_ACTIVE_FLG), +0xf0 free (0 = fixed by NPC_FIX_EVENT), +0xf8 NPC_LOCK_POS words[20]. NPC
  models use the character light set 0x35f6d0 (FF2 GRA3DEMULATIONLIGHTDATACREATIONDATA, PlyrCameraSetLight
  0x21fd28). Talking to an NPC: OK near it opens layout anim 5 with message 0x71 and locks 3 / 0x13 /
  0xf / 0x10 (PlyrCameraTalkLock); one time in four a "chat" line plays under effect 6 instead.
  Model objects are C++: `GameModel` (root, `graphics/game_model.c`, see "Models" under
  "Classes") with a motion object at +4 (RTTI ModelManager / MotionModelManager; MDL_MOT is a typedef,
  and the motion control MOT_CTRL is its BmdMotion) and the model file at +0x14; the player model is a
  `CharPlayerModel` and the player-camera model (vtable 0x341780) a `CharNpcModel` (see "Classes");
  IngameMdlResetAll and friends reset
  the player, player-camera and effect-slot models through motion vtable +0x34 then +0x18(-1, arg).
- Game work `INGAME_WRK` (data 0x327560, 0x4c, `ingame/ingame_wrk.h`): +0 current player number,
  points clamped 0..999999, best photo score, play clock `PLAY_TIME` at 0x327570 (30 frames/s, stops at
  99:59:59; stop flag sdata 0x370ab0), difficulty, current player work pointer 0x32758c, lock table
  0x327590. Outgame flag sdata 0x370ab4; title-menu item-open flags bss 0x3eb368 (backup 0x3eb378).
- Options: `OPTION_WRK` (singleton 0x41e580, at least 0x3c) = `now` + `saved[2]` (`OPTION_DAT`, 0x14:
  volumes 0..19 at +1/+2, `pad_cfg` +8, subtitles +0xc, mono +0x10; the stick readers in unit_ctl.c read
  +0x9 (1: look stick y inverted), +0x12 (0: modes 0/1 take x from the d-pad pressures; 1: mode 5 does;
  non-zero: GetDirTrg uses the stick) and +0x13 (swaps the left / right stick)). saved[0] is taken when an option
  screen opens and restored on cancel (`option_cancel`, sdata 0x3717c8); saved[1] when phase 7 starts.
  Phase-7 state for the screens' files: sdata 0x3717c8..0x3717d8 (`outgame/option.h`).
- Heap: `heapCtrlMalloc/Free` (semaphore-guarded in FF3; HEAP_WRK 0x20: +0x14 live count, +0x18 sema),
  `operator_new/delete` on `GetCppHeapWrkP` (0x329580), `GetSystemHeapWrkP`, `GetIopHeapWrkP` (systemGetMem,
  the EEIOP malloc). EE RAM use: 0x440000/0x640000 packet-buffer halves (2 MB each), 0x840000 C++ heap
  (128 KB), 0x860000 IOP heap (512 KB), 0x8e0000 gra3d packet area (64 KB), 0x104d000 / 0x10be000 font
  packs FONT_TEX_PK4 / FONT_EX_TEX_PK4 (graph2d/font.c, resident from Boot_Load), 0x10ef000 / 0x117b000
  LocalCopy EE buffers 0/1 (screen captures), 0x137c100..0x1fa0000 system heap. All set up by
  `InitSystemHeap` -> `InitCppAndSystemHeap` (sys_heap.c) at the top of InitMainSystem.
- 3D front end `gra3d.c` (0x1da418..0x1dd8b0, FF2's gra3d objectified little): `gra3dInit(0x8e0000,
  0x10000)` from InitGraphics moves three objects into scratchpad (`_ModifyScratchpad`: G3DCOREOBJECT 0xd70
  at 0x700005e0, the VU1 image GRA3DSCRATCHPADLAYOUT 0x440 at 0x70000000 (default data 0x328400), the
  map-shadow image 0x1a0 at 0x70000440), applies the default camera, clears the 39-slot light bank
  (FF3 ranges: directional 0..2, point 3..29, spot 30..38; status 0x20 bytes with a prelight switch at
  +0x10), turns fog on, hands the packet area to gra3dsgdInit and points IG3DCompatible's GS/transform
  hooks (sdata 0x370a3c..) and CVu0Matrix's (0x370aa8) at gra3d. FF3 changes against FF2: the camera
  keeps world->view at +0xe0 (`gra3dApplyCamera(cam, bFixup, bStabilize)`), GS registers go through
  gs_reg.c's cache (g3dSetGsRegister* 0x1cc9b0.. = SetGsRegCached / SetGsRegsCached / GetGsRegCached),
  g3dGeom has `...Up` variants taking an explicit up vector (g3dMatrixSetTargetUp 0x1cd3d0), the two
  light emulators share `gra3dEmulateLightDataSub(dest, src, pos, mag, bChar)`, prelight rotates the
  unit matrix and flips Y/Z afterwards (data 0x329400), and `gra3dSetDrawParam(int, float)` (default
  0, 1.3) feeds `_CalcValidLightIndexByType<4>`.
  Per drawn coordinate the SGD walker (gra3dSGD.c `_SetCoordData` / `SetMaterialDataVU`) has gra3d
  build the VU1 light block (`g3dSetVu1LightData`: lights turned into the object's frame, three per type)
  and the material blocks (`gra3dCalcVu1MaterialData*`, GS colour scales 128/192/255 and 43/86 set by
  `gra3dSetMaterial`); `gra3dSetValidLightId` (0x1dbd10) copies the chosen bank lights into the core's
  nine slots (3 per type). FF3-only: `gra3dSetVu1ConstVF2` / `Get` (0x1dc630 / 0x1dc650) swap the VU1
  image's VF02 (+0x20) around motion/morph draws.
- g3d core `g3dCore.c` (0x1cb890..0x1cced0, FF2's module almost unchanged): one G3DCOREOBJECT (0xd70,
  s_pObject sdata 0x370a24; FF2 0x1170 minus the GS register file) = render/global state, nine light
  slots (`_LIGHTDATA` 0x80, 0..2 dir / 3..5 point / 6..8 spot), ambient +0x4b0, material +0x4c0,
  transforms +0x510, resource +0x680 and FF2's VU1 image (`G3DVU1MEMLAYOUT`, +0x690, same offsets).
  The 21 lighting kernels behind the tables at data 0x359c80..0x359ce0 are instruction-identical to FF2's:
  18 linkonce copies at 0x2ba590..0x2bb108 (no Ghidra functions), 3 point accumulators at 0x1ccaf8..
  FF3 adds a `g3dSetLightStatus` (0x1cde18: loads lAS into t5) before each group in g3dCalcVertexColor
  and uses Vu0Cosf for spot cones. g3dSetRenderState/GetRenderStateRef/SetGlobalState (0x1cbf10..) are
  unreferenced.
- Spot direction sense: two conventions, by path. The VU1 realtime kernel (CalcIntens) dots vDirection
  with light - vertex, so its cone opens along -vDirection, and the realtime installs hand it the
  anti-beam vector: MapDrawSetRoomLight 0x1183e0 negates CharPlayerModel::fl (the beam, from
  CalcLightDir), FodSetSpotLights stores light - interest, _IsBBLightingupSpot 0x1ce0f8 binds the `< 0`
  side. The prelight bake and the emulated-directional path (g3dCalcSpotlightFalloff / g3dCalcAngle)
  read vDirection as the beam; the model's fl reaches characters and map puts only through emulation
  (gra3d.c leaves emulated lights out of the realtime set). The host kernels take the VU1's sense.
- SGD walker `gra3dSGD.c` (0x1e0330..0x1e3850 + linkonce 0x2bcad0..0x2bcb80, FF2's module, written
  2026-10-16, verify): `_gra3dDrawSGD(pSGDTop, SGDRENDERTYPE, pCoord, pnum)` walks a model's block chains
  (header +0x18: uiNumBlock EE addresses; block 0 = textures, last block = skinned meshes drawn by
  SgSortUnitPrimPost) per type: realtime (VU1 packets via g3dDma*, program 0x2df690; preset models
  0x2e11b0 iMT_0 / 0x2e3780 iMT_2/2F, TRI2 units held in save_tri2/bw_pointer until the first visible
  bounding box), prelighting (SgPreRenderPrim: up to three lights per type fed to the g3d core per pass,
  27 point / 9 spot, gra3dIsLightEnableToPrelight), clear, map shadow (AssignShadowPrim for blocks with
  SGDCOORDINATE.bInViewvolume, +0xdc). FF3 changes against FF2: material packets cached in EE memory again
  (SGDMATERIAL.iTagAddressOld/iSizeOld, REF'd when `_IsSameMaterialIndexCache` 0x1e3750 says the light set
  is unchanged; FF2 reset on the DMA toggle), the spot ranking threshold in `_CalcValidLightIndexByType<4>`
  (gra3dGetDrawParamI / F), colour markers (a preset colour whose first word's low half is 1 is never baked
  or overwritten; a baked colour equal to it is bumped by one), category 0x41 ends gra3dsgdDrawPresetDataObject.
  FF3-only: gra3dSetSGDMaterialAlpha / gra3dGetSGDMaterialAlpha 0x1e2f60 / 0x1e2f38 (vDiffuse.w of the
  materials pMaterial..pVectorInfo), SgCalcScreenRectPrim 0x1e2fa8 (gra3dDrawModelCb's walker: WORLDSCREEN /
  WORLDCLIP from the block's coordinate, then gra3dCalcMeshScreenRect 0x1df2d8 on the first mesh), and the
  unreferenced gra3dsgdDrawPresetDataObjectNoClip 0x1e3290. State: sbss 0x37338c.. (save_tri2, save_bw,
  s_ppuhVUVN, s_pCoordBase, s_pSGDTop, edge_check, previous_tri2_prim), CoordCache bss 0x3ee920, skin
  buffers sdata 0x370c80.. (halves of gra3dInit's 0x8e0000 area). PC: SGD pointers stay EE addresses
  (sgd_types.h SgdPtr / SgdAddr; sgdRemap must store EE addresses), the meshes reach the host renderer
  through reipan_graph3d.h hooks placed where MioPan calls its bridge.
- PC host 3D bridge `src/reipan/rendering/reipan_graph3d.cpp` (2026-10-16, MioPan's miopan_graph3d.cpp with
  every SGDSELF32 walk rewritten to EE addresses): preset meshes = the VUVN unit's packed vertices + the mesh
  unit's strips / ST / baked colours (resident GPU meshes per model, TEX0 read from the unit's A+D stream,
  textures resolved from emulated GS memory that the TRI2 / GS image units fill; resolved per model once its
  own TRI2 run is in GS memory, then its TRI2 uploads are skipped); colours = baked + the VU1 kernels
  (gra3dCalcVu1VertexColor) over gra3dSnapshotVu1Lighting, which reads mirrors of the last
  g3dSetVu1LightData / gra3dCalcVu1MaterialData* blocks kept in gra3d.c; realtime meshes the same through the
  _VECTORADDRESS table at VUVN unit + 0x30 (or SetVUVNDataPost's skinned pairs). gra3dApplyCamera hands
  world->view and matViewClipObject to the renderer (reversed-Z there), gra3dApplyFog the fog ramp. Seen
  working on room rs101 only with a temporary debug camera (progress/20261016-1420_reipan_graph3d_debugcam_rs101.png):
  rs101 spans world x -223..229, y -435..50 (-y is up, floor near 0), z 0..795, origin (-132, 0, 490);
  7 preset models (room + shadow source + 5 objects), 232 blocks in the room model.
- SGD data layer `gra3dSGDData.c` (0x1e3850..0x1e4760 + linkonce 0x2bcb80..0x2bcbc0, FF2's module, written
  2026-10-16, verify): `sgdRemap` 0x1e3c28 (callers FUN_002113d0 / FUN_00211450 in unassigned@00210958, the
  model managers) runs once per model (header ucMapFlag): pCoord / pMaterial (if < 0x30000000) and
  pVectorInfo += header address; coordinate pParent index -> address (negative -> 0); the PHEAD's
  HeaderSections SGDVECTORADDRESS entries (stride 0x10 from +4: uiSize, vertex, normal, list) += header; with 4
  sections only the weighted vertex lists survive (none when gra3d's blend buffer is 0 or the weighted pools are
  absent), MappingVertexList gives each run its vOff into the blend buffer ("VNBuffer Over size" drops the
  lists); block heads += header; each unit's pNext is relative to the unit itself; material units' index ->
  pMaterial + i * 0xb0; a realtime VUVN unit's _VECTORADDRESS indices (pPUHead + 3) -> unique pool + i*0x10,
  weighted pool + i*0x20, or the global blend buffers + i*0x10 (vector type 2/3 with lists). The last unit of
  a chain (pNext 0) is never looked at. RebuildTRI2Files 0x1e3890 is dead: its switch sgdEnableOptimizeTexture
  0x1e4538 (sdata 0x370cb0) has no callers. sgdRemapInverse 0x1e42e8 (no callers) undoes header / parents /
  materials / chains only. Bone matrices: sgdCalcCoordinateMatrix 0x1e4070 (parents first, bCalc +0xd4;
  callers alg_manage.c / motion.c), sgdCalcBoneCoordinate 0x1e3fa8, sgdCalcCoordinate 0x1e4018 (root matCoord
  = the model's matrix; from gra3dExecPrelightMatrix); flag clears 0x1e3850 / 0x1e41a8 / 0x1e41d0 / 0x1e41e8.
  sgdVerifyLightData 0x1e40f8 (MapDrawInitRoom): room light file ZERO2LIGHTDATAFILE 0x1b00, its
  GRA3DLIGHTDATA at +0x4f0; point lights get vDirection 0, all fFalloff 1.0. sgdGetBoundingBox 0x1e4230 merges
  every bounding-box unit's 8 corners (at pPUHead + 1) into a box that also holds the origin
  (sgdExpandBoundingBox 0x1e4540; MapPutGetBoundingBox, FUN_00183d00).
- g3d geometry `g3dGeom.c` (0x1cd180..0x1cde18, FF2's module minus asserts, written 2026-10-16): the
  coordinate-matrix builders gra3d's camera uses (SetPosition/Direction/Target/Roll, rows 0..2 = right, up,
  forward, row 3 = position), FF3-only g3dMatrixSetDirectionUp 0x1cd300 / SetTargetUp 0x1cd3d0 (up hint as a
  vector), InverseTransform (gra3dShadow), planes, ray/sphere and ray/ellipsoid hits. RotationByAxis calls
  `motQuaternion2Matrix` 0x2bb108 (right after the g3dCore linkonce kernels; also called by motion code
  0x2133d8.. and 0x1de8c8; unassigned, stub in unassigned_stubs.c, prototype in motion.h). g3dCalcEllipseEffectiveRadius 0x1cdb90 has no callers.
- Files: `LoadReq(file_no, addr)` -> `FileLoadReqEE`; `GetFileSize`; cddat table. The loader
  (`system/eeiop/fileload.c`, 0x268e70..0x269fa0, FF2's module) is a queue of 50 `FILE_LOAD_WRK`
  (0x28: file, sector, buffer, callback, type, priority, arg, read, size, temp_id, slot id) served by the
  thread `thFileLoad` 0x269d40: sort by priority then sector, fire the tail (lowest priority value) at the
  IOP over SIF RPC 3 (`rpcFileLoadReqSub`, 0x40-byte command at 0x40e240 = 7-word `LOAD_REQ_NEW`, no file
  name any more), compressed files through cmp_eeiop's decode thread. Load ids are slot << 16 | temp_id.
  `FILE_LOAD_SYS` at bss 0x40e1c0 gained +0x28 `use_cd_image` (EEIOP_DEF.cd_dat_name set: FileLoadReq
  asks GetFileStartSector, else sector 0). Raw loads size with GetFileSize, compressed with GetFileCmpSize.
  Started by ee_iopInit -> ee_iopInitSub -> FileLoadInit. The file table is `system/eeiop/cddat.c`
  (0x26f340..0x26f770, reconstructed): ee_iopInit reads ZERO3.FHD whole into ee_iopMalloc memory (after
  SndInit, and after sending def->cd_dat_name to the IOP as command 3) and calls `cddatInit(def->pc_path,
  image)` 0x26f3d8 -> `cddatSetHeader` 0x26f340, which keeps the image at gp-0xce0 and turns its table
  offsets into pointers in place (`CDDAT_HEADER`, cddat.h; ReiPan keeps the offsets). Root path
  `title_root_path[200]` at 0x410fb0, GetFileName's buffer at 0x410eb0. Without +0x24 use_cd_image but
  with names, GetFileSize/GetFileCmpSize ask the host (`QueryFileSize` 0x26b4d0, empty in retail).
- EE<->IOP layer `system/eeiop/ee_iop.c` (0x26ad30..0x26b3f0, FF2's ee_iop.c, reconstructed): ee_iopInit
  0x26af28 (reboot, HIL/DIL IRX load, ee_iopInitSub 0x26ad30 = bind RPC 1 + REQ_IOP_REBOOT round trip +
  ee_iopQueryInit + FileLoadInit + FileStreamInit 0x270a48 (RPC 4), SndInit, REQ_SET_CD_DAT, FHD load).
  malloc64/free64 at gp-0x53c/-0x540, queue offset gp-0x544; `iop_com_buffer[0x200]` 0x40ec80 (FF2 had
  4 KB), `IOP_RET_STATUS iop_ret` 0x40ee80 (0x1a8), reply buffer 0x40f040, `sif_cli_data` 0x40f200.
  FF3 dropped FF2's GetVoiceNowAdrs/GetVoiceLoopAdrs and every print/assert.
- Query RPC `system/eeiop/ee_iop_q.c` (0x26b3f0..0x26b610, FF2's ee_iop_q.c, reconstructed 2026-10-20):
  RPC 2, one synchronous sceSifCallRpc per request under `query_sema_id` gp-0x548; `sif_cli_data_q`
  0x40f640, reply `iop_query_buffer[0x200]` 0x40f240, argument `iop_query_arg[0x200]` 0x40f440. fno =
  EE_IOP_QUERY_ENUM (ee_iop_q.h): 1 ReqQueryLoadCancel (IOP ClearLoadReq), 2 / 3 ReqQuerySPUTransCoreGet /
  Release (StartMovie / EndMovie claim an SPU core from the IOP's WaitSpuTransSema pool for the movie
  audio's auto-DMA). QueryFileSize 0x26b4d0 (fno 0) is an empty body in retail.
- Sound front end `system/eeiop/snd.c` (0x26f770..0x26fd50, FF2's snd.c, reconstructed; runs on PC since
  ee_iop.c: SndInit from ee_iopInit, SndMain from ee_iopMain). `SOUND_SYS snd_sys` 0x411078 (mono, type_vol[5]),
  effect_mode/effect_adrs gp+0x5a0/+0x5a8, eff_use_size_tbl 0x333a90 (FF2's values). SndInit takes the
  SPU RAM range from EEIOP_DEF +0x3c/+0x40 (`spu_mem_start` 0x5010, `spu_mem_size` 0x1faff0). Around it:
  snd_3d.c (Snd3DInit 0x26eb88 .. Snd3DMain 0x26f288) and `system/eeiop/snd_pcmstream.c` (0x26fd50..0x270240,
  FF2's straight PCM streamer, reconstructed 2026-10-20): `SND_PCM_STREAM_WRK snd_pcm_stream_wrk[2]` data
  0x333ab8 (0x28 each: PCM_STREAM_PLAY 4 + PCM_STREAM_START 0x10 -- FF2's file name gone, so the IOP side's
  payload is 0x10 too -- + status, flags +0x18 bit 0 pre_load_ok / 1 play_flg / 2 pause, file_no, vol /
  target_vol / spd). Only SndPCMStreamInit (SndInit) and SndPCMStreamMain (SndMain) have callers in FF3;
  Start / Play / Stop / AllStop / Pause / Restart / IsUse / IsPreload / Fade are unreferenced, so no PCM
  stream ever plays (movie audio goes elsewhere). FF3 inlines Fade into Play and puts Fade last. IOP command
  numbers are FF2's IOP_COMMAND_ENUM (ee_iop.h).
- IOP side `src/system/iop/` (PC only; 2026-10-14): MioPan's port of FF2's iopsys.irx stands in for FF3's
  ZEROIRX module. start() (called from ReiPan_IopBoot on the first SIF bind) registers RPC 1 (iopCommand:
  walks the EE's command queue by payload size, answers IOP_RET_STATUS), REQ_IOP_REBOOT creates RPC 2
  (query), RPC 3 (file load, unused on PC), RPC 4 (file stream) and the CD reader. Streams: StreamStart
  reads from IMG_BD.BIN (lsn of REQ_SET_CD_DAT's file + start_sector), reports ST_STREAM_PRE_LOAD then
  ST_STREAM_PLAYING once the ring is filled; StreamPlay keys the voices into sdk/iop_voice.cpp's mixer.
  Title BGM: BGM_00_TITLE_STR (0xfa9, 1515520 bytes, HXD 0xfaa: 2 ch, interleave 0x800, 44100 Hz, no loop
  points, about 30 s).
- ADPCM streams `system/eeiop/snd_stream.c` (0x26b610..0x26c8c0, FF2's snd_stream.c, reconstructed
  2026-10-13): `SND_STREAM_WRK snd_stream_wrk[2]` 0x40f680 (0xc0 each: STREAM_PLAY 0x1c + STREAM_START
  0x3c -- FF2's 256-byte file name is gone -- + SOUND_INFO[2] + state; flags word +0x94 bit 6 = FF3's
  `loop` from SndStreamStart's 5th argument, gating the header loop points), header_buf_64[2][0x800]
  0x40f800, irq_core_source[2] gp+0x550 (0x373a40), load priority gp+0x558. FF3 orders it FadeStop,
  Fade, AllStop 0x26b740, Init, StartHeaderOnMemory (unreferenced), Start, ...; SndStreamSetPosition
  0x26c658 is unreferenced. SndAllStop and SndBgmStopAll call SndStreamAllStop only;
  SndPCMStreamAllStop 0x2700b0 exists but nothing calls it.
- After the sound front end, FF3 links FF2's iopsys.a tail in this order (all reconstructed 2026-10-13):
  `cmp_eeiop.c` 0x270240..0x2705e0 (decode thread: tmp buffer 0x16000 + stack 0x1800 from FileLoadInit's
  buffer, thread id sdata 0x37281c = -1, semas gp+0x5b8 decode end / gp+0x5bc block arrived, SIF cmd
  handler intr_cmp 0x270358 on sys cmd buffer 0x411090, decode_load_ee 0x411150; FF3 drops
  cmp_eeiopIsLate), `snd_util.c` 0x2705e0 (AUTO_BD_WRK 0x60 x EEIOP_DEF auto_bd_wrk_num, gp+0x5c0/+0x5c4),
  `file_stream.c` 0x270a48 (RPC 4 client 0x411200, arg 0x4111c0 = LOAD_DEF_STRUCT without file name,
  reply 0x411180; only user my_strfile MyStrRead), `spu_mem.c` 0x270cf8 (SPU_BUF[50] x 0x18 at 0x411240,
  sema gp+0x5cc; release by predicate 0x270f80 / 0x2710e0; FF2's spu_mem.o sat before snd_buffer, FF3
  moved it here; hxd.o and DrawSPUMemory are gone), `spu_voice.c` 0x2711f0 (core_voices[2] sdata
  0x372820, 24 voices per core). Then FF2's encodes.a, unchanged (2026-10-21, MioPan port): `encodes.c`
  0x2712a0..0x271b08 (Okumura LZSS: SlideEncode, SlideDecode, init_tree / insert_node / delete_node; dad
  data 0x333b08, lson 0x335b10, rson 0x337b18, text 0x339d20, matchpos / matchlen sdata 0x372828 / 0x37282a)
  and `cmp.c` 0x271b08..0x271c30 (CMP_DecodeOne / CMP_Decode / CMP_Init; CMP_Decode has no ROM caller, the
  host loader uses it for every compressed file).
- GS/2D: `SetGsRegCached(data, reg, 1)` / `SetGsRegsCached(pairs, n, 1)` send one A+D packet through the
  packet buffer and keep each value in a 128-entry cache (0x3eed30); `GetGsRegCached(reg)` reads it back
  (save / change / restore idiom), `SetDefaultGsRegs` restores alpha/test/TEX1/scissor/zbuf of both
  contexts. `StartDmaDirectTrans/EndDmaDirectTrans`, FF2's `DispSprD`, `DispSqrD`, `CopySprDToSpr` family,
  `LocalCopyLtoB/BtoL/LtoL` (g2d_draw; copy table `scpw` at 0x327438, 7 geometries: FF2's six plus
  6 = 320x224 -> 320x224).
- 2D primitives (`graphics/graph2d/g2d_draw.c`, 0x1c8e28..0x1cae98, FF2 order: InitG2DDraw, CopySprDToSpr,
  DispSprD, CopySqrDToSqr, CopySq4DToSqr, DispSqrD, CopySprDToSpr2, DispSprD2, LocalCopy*, PK2DKick,
  Start/EndDmaDirectTrans, SetDmaDirectImageTrans). FF3 dropped FF2's PK2D ring and SetDrawEnv: each
  Disp* sends its draw state with one `SetGsRegsCached` (sprites: ALPHA_1, TEX1_1, CLAMP_1, TEST_1,
  ZBUF_1, TEXA; squares: ALPHA_1, TEST_1, ZBUF_1, TEXA), then one DIRECT packet. No interlace halving
  (FF2's gInterlace is gone): pixel = coordinate, GS window = 2048 + x - 320 with XYOFFSET 1728,1824.
  Only PSMT4 gets the T8+CLD texture load. Copy* defaults: TEXA AEM/TA1 0x80, ALPHA (Cs-Cd)*0x80+Cd,
  ZBUF 0x118 Z16S masked, TEST ALWAYS/ALWAYS, z = 0xfffff - (pri & 0xfffff), PRIM 0x154. FF3 has no
  CopyGSqDToSqr / CopyGS4DToSqr / SetPanel. `InitG2DDraw` only calls FadeCtrlInit.
- Screen fade (`graphics/graph2d/fade.c`, 0x1cae98..0x1cb110, FF2's fade.c, 2026-10-15 verify):
  `fade_ctrl` FADE_MODE_CTRL 0x327550 (0xc: state 0 idle / 1 in / 3 out, alpha 0x80 = covered, step,
  r, g, b). FadeCtrlInit 0x1cae98 (alpha 0x80: black until the first FadeInReq), FadeMain 0x1caed8
  (640x448 DispSqrD at the alpha before the step; FF3 lost the ERROR printf), GetFadeState 0x1cafe8
  (1 = idle; was named FadeIsEnd), FadeInReq 0x1caff8 / FadeOutReq 0x1cb068 (r, g, b, frames u_int:
  0 = at once, > 0x80 = step 1), FF3-only GetFadeAlpha 0x1cb0e8 (alpha, or 0x80 - alpha). Not on the
  boot / title path: FadeMain runs in-game (IngameDrawSub, candle, mission, save/load, start menu).
- 2D helpers (`graphics/graph2d/g2d_debug.c`, 0x1c83d0..0x1c8e28, FF2's g2d_debug.o slot, FF3-only code):
  `DispSqrRect(x0, y0, x1, y1, pri, r, g, b, a)` 0x1c8528 (absolute corners in pixels, ZMSK 1) over
  `DispSqrRectZmsk` 0x1c83d0; quads `DispSqr4Zmsk` 0x1c8560 / `DispSqr4` 0x1c86f8; `SetLine2D(x0, y0,
  x1, y1, pri, r, g, b, a)` 0x1c87d0 — **centre-relative** coordinates ((x + 2048) * 16, no -320), ALPHA
  (Cs-Cd)*FIX+Cd with FIX = a, TEST 0x5000d, ZBUF 0x10a000118, one LINE DIRECT packet; `SetLine2DShade`
  0x1c8968, `SetCircle2D` 0x1c8c98, FF2's `SetLine2DPacket` 0x1c8b78 (pixel coordinates, z 1);
  `DispZbufView(tbp)` 0x1c8730 (a page as PSMZ16S, full screen). Only DispSqrRect has callers. The two
  functions before it, 0x1c8388 / 0x1c83b0 (in .ctors at 0x2e9150), are foot_se.c's static
  initialiser (FootSe_static_init: zero the footstep file mask 0x3732a8 / 0x3732ac).
  PC: Disp* draw through the renderer's quad bridge; `SetGsRegCached(s)` shadow ALPHA_1 / TEST_1 / ZBUF_1
  / SCISSOR_1 into the renderer (`GsRegShadow`), so any FF3 draw-state write reaches it.
  LocalCopy*: `LtoBAdrs` stores PSMCT32 via sceGsExecStoreImage (0x2872f0) after sceGsSyncPath +
  FlushCache; `BtoLAdrs` sends <=100-line strips (BITBLTBUF/TRXPOS/TRXREG/TRXDIR + SetDmaDirectImageTrans;
  unknown types = 640x448 PSMCT24); `LtoL` draws the source page (CT24) as one FST SPRITE into the
  destination (FRAME CT24, XYOFFSET 0x6c08/0x7908, regs ALPHA/TEX1/CLAMP/TEST from data 0x359bf8 + ZBUF
  0x118 masked) then restores FRAME_1 = draw buffer `(count & 1) * 0x8c`, XYOFFSET 0x6c00/0x7200. FF3 LtoL
  hard-codes that ZBUF (FF2 kept the live ZBP). The photo code (0x227f98, photo_dat area) does LtoL 5 of the
  shown frame into 0x2bc0, then `FUN_0022b1a0`: LtoB(3, 1, 0x2bc0) and reads EE 0x117b000 on the CPU.
  PC bridge (MioPan's): frame -> page/stash = renderer capture slot keyed by that address; LOCAL -> HOST
  of a frame buffer into a non-stash buffer, or of a page registered by LtoL, = frame read-back
  (`ReiPan_RendererReadbackScreen`); BtoL of a non-stash buffer = re-issued `ReiPan_GsUpload`.
- VRAM (GS block addresses): frame buffers 0 and 0x1180 (640x448 CT32; the one on screen is
  `((count + 1) & 1) * 0x1180`), Z buffer 0x2300 (ZBP 0x118, Z16S), free texture area from 0x2bc0.
- Screen capture (gs_reg.c): `ScreenCaptureSave(type, blend)` copies the frame on screen to EE 0x10ef000
  (type 0 full, 1 shrunk 320x224, 2 width halved on the EE); `ScreenCaptureDisp(r, g, b, alpha)` uploads it
  to 0x2bc0 and draws it full screen each frame. Used by the Story pause/menu/save/overlay phases.
- C++ runtime (identified, library): `_Unwind_*`, `__cxa_*`, `std_terminate`, basic_string helpers, locale.

## Classes (RTTI)

`python tools/reverse/rtti.py` lists all 113 polymorphic classes (name, base, vtable, slots,
ctors/dtors; `--class NAME`, `--vtable ADDR`; data in work/classes.json). The trees that matter most:

- Models: `GameModel` (0x341f80) -> `GameMotionModel` (0x341f40) -> `GameAnimeModel` (0x341ef0) ->
  `CharModel` (0x341710) -> `CharPlayerModel` (0x341800), `CharNpcModel` (0x341780), `CharEnemyModel`
  (0x341690), `CharCatModel` (0x3415e0); `GameModel` -> `AcsModel` (accessories), `FlyModel`, `PssModel`;
  `GameMotionModel` -> `SceneModel`.
  `graphics/game_model.c` (0x1d2a40..0x1d36f0, inline virtuals 0x2ac568..0x2ac590 / 0x2ac980):
  GameModel (0x1c: +4 the motion object `MDL_MOT`, +8 the builder, +0xc two free hooks
  (SavePointFreeEnemies 0x24e0f8 / SavePointFreeEnemy10a 0x24e158: release enemies when the file
  cache is full, then the request is retried), +0x14 model file id, +0x18 its data from the shared
  file cache 0x32d700). Vtable: +0 Build (a Normal- or MotionModelBuilder over the PK4, by whether
  it has a cls member; the director 0x20ee10 runs builder +8/+0xc/+0x10; builder +0x14 = the motion
  object; motion -1 starts when cls exists), +4 BuildPre, +8/+0xc dtors, +0x10 IsLoaded (file in ->
  Build once), +0x14 IsDrawn (1), +0x18 SetWorldMatrix (-> mot +8), +0x1c GetWorldMatrix (unit;
  AcsModel: its coordinate), +0x20 DrawMdlPre, +0x24 / +0x28 Draw's two steps, +0x2c GetCoordPos,
  +0x30 GetCoordAxis, +0x34 GetJointCoord (0; CharModel 0x145528). Per frame: UpdateMatrix 0x1d2d50
  (+0x1c -> +0x18), DrawMdl 0x1d2d98 (gra3dSetGsRegisterDefault, +0x20, ModelManager::Draw 0x2106c0). The
  motion object's +4 (`MDL_OBJ` = ModelObject, `graphics/model_object.c`) carries the SGDs: SetAlphaAll /
  SetAlpha (0x211db8 / 0x211e20, material alpha), GetCoord (coordinates, 0xe0 each, matLocalWorld at +0x40),
  vtable +0x14 / +0x18 the texture frames (MultiTextureModelObject's per-SGD blend alpha of the mlt texture). GameMotionModel (0x28): +0x1c motion file, +0x1e second
  ("shp") file, +0x20 / +0x24 their data; Load(mdl, mot, shp) 0x1d33d8, Build 0x1d34b0 always a
  MotionModelBuilder with Pk4GetData(mot, 0) and the shp data; IsLoaded 0x1d35b8 waits for all three
  (CharModel / CharEnemyModel / CharNpcModel use it). `graphics/game_anime_model.c` (0x1d36f0..0x1d3b40):
  GameAnimeModel (0x30) adds the anime script player AnimeCodeManagerGame at +0x2c and its handler
  AnimeCodeInterpretorGame at +0x28 (the player's handler too); Build 0x1d3a48 = GameMotionModel's,
  then its new vtable +0x38 (CreateAnimeManager, new 0x58) and +0x3c (CreateAnimeHandler, new 0xc
  over mot and the player); +0x40 StartAnime 0x1d38b0 (-> AnimeCodeManagerGame::Start; CharModel
  overrides it with 0x1450e0); SetWorldMatrix 0x1d3958 (+0x18) runs the script each frame: Advance
  the clock unless the motion control (mot +0x40) blends (+0x24), the player's Step, then GameModel's.
  Accessors on the player: GetAnimeLength 0x1d38e0 (ScrPlayGetLength 0x1295d8), GetAnimeTime 0x1d3910
  (+0x38 clock), IsAnimeEnd 0x1d3928 (cursor +8, 1 until a start), GetAnimeNo 0x1d3940 (scr_no as a
  halfword), SetAnimeSpeed 0x1d39f0 (mot vtable +0x24 and the clock's speed), GetAnimeFlag 0x1d3b20
  (flag[n], one step long).
  `ingame/char_model.c` (0x1441d8..0x1479b0, 2026-10-16): CharModel (0x3c) adds +0x30 chr (row of the
  joint map 0x2f5830 [47][29] and the box table 0x34fe20), +0x32 model type (its anime script table
  0x2f57a8, 33 entries, given to AnimeCodeManager::scr_tbl by Build 0x1451a0; type > 32 uses 32), +0x34
  CharRmdData (root moves; motion PK4 member 1), +0x38 CharWatchControl (the head watch). Vtable
  slots after GameAnimeModel's: +0x44 CreateWatchControl, +0x48 CreateShapes (player only), +0x4c
  CreateRmdData, +0x50 StopShapes (StartAnime 0x1450e0 runs it first), +0x54 / +0x58 GetBounds /
  GetBoundsDraw, +0x5c CanRootMove, +0x60 GetWalkStep. IsDrawn (+0x14) is the box in view
  (CheckModelBoundingBox); the handler is an AnimeCodeInterpretorChar (0x10, +0xc owner) whose
  command 3 plays footsteps / voices. CharPlayerModel (0x1c0) keeps the flashlights fl / fl2 /
  reflectionlight (+0x50 / +0xc0 / +0x130, UpdateLight 0x146cb8 copies light-set slot 38 and aims
  it along the look), foot / arm IK (0x1466e8 / 0x146ac8), the blink / face / hand CharPlayerShapes
  (+0x40..+0x48) and the dream light flag +0x1bc. CharEnemyModel (0x48) saves its bones' world
  matrices each frame (+0x40, system heap) and reads joints from them; CharNpcModel (0x40) and
  CharCatModel (0x44, two per cat: part 1 hangs from part 0's joint 0x1c) take pos / rot from their
  works. Every Char*::GetWorldMatrix is RotY(rot y - pi) (enemies RotZ, -X, Y) placed at the work's
  position. The helpers (CharWatchControl, CharRmdData, CharPlayerShape and its stateless
  singleton states) belong to ingame/enemy/alg_manage.c (0x1479b0..).
- Model data: `ModelObject` (0x3421a0) -> `TextureModelObject` -> `Multi/DummyTextureModelObject`;
  `ModelManager` (0x3420e0) -> `MotionModelManager`; `ModelBuilder` -> `NormalModelBuilder` ->
  `MotionModelBuilder`, `PssModelBuilder`.
  The build (graphics/model_builder.c, 0x20df28..0x20f388 + inlines 0x2bd118..0x2bd7b0, 2026-10-16): a
  one-word director (operator new(4), ModelDirectorConstruct 0x20ee10) calls the builder's vtable +8
  BuildObject, +0xc BuildTexture, +0x10 BuildMotion; +0x14 GetResult is GameModel.mot. Model PK4 members
  (names at data 0x32d6e0): 0 mpk, 1 tpk, 2 mlt, 3 ccs, 4 cld, 5 cls, 6 mono, 7 flgs. BuildObject: no tpk
  -> ModelObject(mpk, flgs) (0x211450, 0x10, sgdRemap of every SGD), tpk -> TextureModelObject (0x18,
  inlined: + tpk, mono), tpk + mlt -> MultiTextureModelObject (0x212010, 0x20); cld -> ModelCollision
  (0x14) on MorphGetCoord(obj, 0). NormalModelBuilder::BuildMotion -> ModelManager(obj, collision)
  (0x2105c0, 0xc: +4 obj, +8 collision, registered in ModelCollisionSet 0x32d6c0; graphics/mmanage.c). MotionModelBuilder::BuildTexture: ccs -> ModelCcs (0x20f7b8, 0x60, no
  vtable); mot (motion PK4) -> BmdData of the entry whose bone count (short +8) is the SGD's uiNumBlock - 2
  (else entry 0) + BmdMotion(obj, data, mot); shp -> ShpData + per entry ShpMotion (type 0) or
  ShpPhysicsMotion (type 1, started at once via VertexMotion::Play 0x212a40), motions of one mesh
  (ShpMotion::GetMeshNo 0x2172c8) share one vertex buffer (SetVertexBuf 0x217130); cls -> ClothData +
  ClothMotion per cloth (Init 0x214ce8). Its BuildMotion -> MotionModelManager (0x2108b8, 0x24, nine
  arguments). The builder owns all of it (GameModel's destructor deletes the builder).
  Collision shapes (cld: 0x10 header {num, mask}, 0x30 shapes {type, coord[2] shorts, size, push, p[2]}):
  type 0 sphere, 1 capsule (0x2bd3a8; past an end FF3 adds the body radius twice), 2 plane (normal p[0],
  stored with y / z negated through 0x329400, through world p[1]); a body (pos +0, move +0x20, off +0x40,
  mask +0x48, radius +0x4c) is pushed out, and back along its move by push when push > 1e-5. HitCheck
  0x20f1c0 runs only while sdata 0x37126c (draw_en_37126c, ingame.c) is set.
- Motion (`graphics/motion/motion.c`, 0x2125f8..0x217c90 + inline virtuals 0x2bd8c0..0x2be0e0, 2026-10-16):
  `Motion` (0x342230) -> `BoneMotion` (0x342200) -> `BmdMotion` (0x342270, 0x50); `Motion` -> `VertexMotion`
  (0x3421d8) -> `ShpMotion` (0x342368, 0x38) -> `ShpPhysicsMotion` (0x342338, 0x70), `VertexMotion` ->
  `ClothMotion` (0x3422e0, 0x300); data `MotionData` -> `BmdData` (0x14), `ShpData` (8), `ClothData` (8).
  Motion: +4 ModelObject, +8 frame (SetFrame clamps at 0), +0xc speed (1.0; a step is speed *
  GetFrameTimeScale); vtable +8 Step (1 at the end), +0xc unused, +0x10 IsEnd. BoneMotion: +0x10 motion
  PK4, +0x14 a second PK4 searched when a member is no BMD (never set in code read so far), +0x18 no,
  +0x1c the overshoot past the end (the anime script starts the next motion from it), +0x20 blend
  counter, +0x24 blending, +0x28 blend frames; vtable +0x14 Pose(world matrix), +0x18 SetMotion(no),
  +0x1c EndBlend, +0x20 BlendPose, +0x24 GetFrameNum; Update 0x212840 = pose (or blend pose) + step.
  BmdMotion: +0x2c/+0x30 the pose a blend starts from (rotations / translations, a vector a bone),
  +0x34/+0x38 the pose drawn last (ResetPose copies them over, PlayMotion calls it), +0x3c BmdData,
  +0x40 / +0x44 root yaws, +0x48 / +0x4c a yaw callback (f12 yaw, a0 arg; nothing sets it yet). Pose:
  per bone slerp of the two keys around frame (keys every frame, or every second when head +0xa),
  translations lerped, into SGD 0's coordinate bone+1 (motQuaternion2Matrix, row 3 += t); the
  coordinates are linked by the BMD parent table at construction (bone parent -1 -> coordinate 0).
  Only when the BMD's bone count == SGD 0's uiNumBlock - 2. BMD entry: "BMD\0", +4 frames, +8 bones,
  +0xa half rate, +0xb packed quaternions (4 x u16: bit 14 = 1.0, else /16384, bit 15 sign), +0xd
  translation mode (2: keys > 0 hold root bones only), +0xf relocated flag, +0x10 parents, then
  the key table (relocated in place to EE addresses once) and the keys (rotations then translations).
  VertexMotion: +0x10 playing, +0x14 loop, +0x18 reverse, +0x1c hold (frame frozen, never ends:
  MotionModelManager::SetShapeLoop), +0x20 stop at the end, +0x24 the shape / cloth no; Play 0x212a40
  takes (loop, stop_end, reverse, frame, speed); vtable +0x14 Stop, +0x18 Pose, +0x1c GetFrameNum(no).
  ShpMotion: +0x28 ShpData, +0x2c the mesh's base vertices (shared per mesh), +0x30 own copy, +0x34
  dirty; Pose adds each track's weighted delta set onto PHEAD.pUniqVertex of the SGD (WriteVertex puts
  the base back first). ShpData = PK4 {0: motions {tracks, type, frames, mesh; tracks of 0x10 +
  frames weights}, 1: delta sets {num; vectors with w = vertex index}}. ShpPhysicsMotion: a damped
  spring (+0x40 offset in [-1, 1], +0x50 last point, +0x60 velocity) driven by one coordinate's
  movement, shown through six delta sets (+-x, +-y, +-z). ClothMotion (written 2026-10-16): a
  mass-spring cloth on one mesh, w x h points x layers, one point (MDL_CLD_BODY 0x50, the chains'
  collision body) a unique vertex. ClothData = PK4, one member a cloth: +0xc mesh, +0x10 file param
  {type 0..5, preset, w, h, bone[2], stiff[2], damp, gravity[3], angle (deg), mask, -, num_pin, pin
  columns from the right}; CopyParam 0x214e28 rearranges it into a 0x40 heap CLOTH_PARAM (+0x1c mask,
  +0x20 stiff, +0x28 damp, +0x2c angle, +0x30 gravity); presets 0x35caa0 x 3 {stiff 0.38/0.45/0.43, 0.05,
  damp 0.8/0.9/0.78, gravity y 0.11/0.04/0.18}. Types: 1/4 a tube (first-last row links), 2/5 two bones
  (pinned points below the middle row follow bone 1; layer-to-layer links; bone 1's frame turned by
  angle about z), 3..5 follow half the root's movement. Links 0xc {u16 a, b, rest len, kind 0}. Fields:
  +0x38 param, +0x3c points, +0x40 links, +0x4c stepped, +0x50 reset_req, +0x54 calc off (no writer),
  +0x58 settled, +0x5c written, +0x60 mat[9] (last world / rotation / inverse / inverse-rotation pairs,
  bone 1 in bone 0's space at +0x260), +0x2a0 vec[3] (root position for the jump check, its xz
  direction for the turn check, root position for the follow), wind +0x2d0..+0x2fc. Pose 0x2162b8:
  first call 25 (PAL) / 30 settle steps and nothing drawn; then Calc(1) a frame (skipped when written,
  draw_en_371348 0 and mask bit 2), the deferred Reset (resettled unless IsMoviePhase and type 2),
  WriteVertex 0x2161d8. Calc 0x2156c8: points live in bone space; to the world with last matrices,
  root follow, CalcWind 0x2bdd90 (gust cycle period x 0.85..1.15, sine rise / calm / 0.3 fall, half the
  points at random), gravity, two CalcLink 0x2bdba0 passes (stiffness push, halved past 1.3x rest) each
  with model_collision_set.HitCheck(point, 0), back with new matrices, pinned points at rest, velocity
  damped (y x 0.88 more); a root jump > 3.2 (types 3..5) / 10 or xz turn > 0.3 rad sets reset_req.
  Also in motion.c's range: ModelObject::IkLimb 0x2165d8 (two-bone IK about z after taking out the
  root's yaw; caller 0x1466e8) and ModelObject::IkChain 0x216c40 (CCD up the pParent chain about one
  axis, cosine threshold 0.99999, <= 101 joints; caller 0x146ac8), both char_model.c.
- G3D render state: `IG3DCompatible` -> `IAutoState` -> `CAutoTransform<G3DTRANSFORMSTATETYPE 0>`,
  `CAutoGsRegisters<3>`; `IG3DCompatible` -> `CRenderTarget`; `IG3DResource` -> `CTexture` (0x341e58).
- 2D / UI: `CSprite` (0x341e48), `Menu_DrawTex` -> `Menu_DrawTexMulti`, `ScrollBar` -> `B2d_ScrollBar1/3`,
  `CB2dBase` -> `CmenuCamBase` -> `CmenuCamBasic/StLens/Stat` (the camera menu, phase 0x39 from the
  file menu: CmenuCamMain 0x112150 holds a frame CmenuCamBase (layout 0x1240 + language), page[3] =
  Stat / Basic / StLens switched with out_etc / in_etc, the HUD layout (HUD_LAYOUT, HudLayout*
  0x1503c8..0x150ac0; IsReady waits on HudLayoutIsReady) and a background CB2dBase (0x1254); singleton sdata gp-0x7e00; page virtual
  slots +8 Step (nonzero ends the menu), +0xc Draw, +0x10 SetHooks, +0x14 Load, +0x18 Enter, +0x1c
  Setup; CmenuCamStat 0x1146a8..0x116fa0 is the status page: layout 0x124f + language, a state
  machine (step table 0x34daf0: top / film / slot / function / lens list) over five CSR_GRID cursors
  (0x10aac0..: two axes {min, max, cur, skip mask}, wrap bits, moved bits, flags, move SE; CsrGridMove
  per frame), a B2D_SEL row highlight (0x109288..: the selected row copies a cursor item's alpha),
  two LERP_F blinking the cursors 1.0 <-> 0.3125 over 40 frames, 52 item hooks (list 0x34d590) and
  the lens table 0x34d968 {Rei lens, Kei lens, item} that CmenuCamStLens shares; help texts are
  MenuWdwSetMsg(0x5a, n) by {key, msg1} tables (MenuWdwSetMsgByTable 0x1120e8); CmenuCamBasic
  0x1106d8..0x112150 is the Capabilities page: layout 0x1245 + language (EDIT_PHF), one CSR_GRID
  (x -1 / +1 pages, y the CamLvGetKind rows 0..2 + the special slots) and a STEP_SEQ (+0x38, owner the
  page) of four steps: top, the yes/no (yes: "shine" on CmenuCamMain's bg at the next pip,
  IngameWrkSubPoint(CamLvGetCost)), the max / short-of-points notice, and the shine wait that runs
  CamLvUp on frame 0x22; the rows table 0x34c990 (0x28 each: row, name / lv / frame / pip / pts items,
  lit and plain names, the cost number item, UP_COST id 2000 + row) drives 33 item hooks (list 0x34c770);
  help texts MenuWdwSetMsg(0x68, n); CmenuCamStLens 0x112db0..0x1146a8 is the Upgraded Lens page: layout
  0x124a + language (LENS_PHF), the owned lenses (lens table order, column b00 != 0) as a MENU_CSR_WRK list
  of 6 view rows with a B2d_ScrollBar3 and two B2D_GRID_SPR cursor rows (0x436 / 0x438, step 0x1f), the
  same four steps plus an empty-list step, lens levels via LENS_WRK GetLevel / LevelUp (max 3) and costs
  UpCostGet(b00, lens, lv) -- the lens numbers 0..8 are UP_COST ids; singleton sdata 0x36b718), `CmemShelt` (0x3410a0: heap snapshot buffer; CExtraMissionShelt and the retry backup
  0x10b9a8 / restore 0x10ba58 use it), `CWindowMsg` (0x341090), `CTutorial`
  (0x341c20), `capCtl` (0x341560, the fade layer), `IEventGraph2D` -> `CandleControl` (0x341520).
- Anime code: `AnimeCodeManager` -> `AnimeCodeManagerGame`; `AnimeCodeInterpretor` ->
  `AnimeCodeInterpretorGame` -> `AnimeCodeInterpretorChar`.
- Player shape/face/hand states (`CharPlayerShape*`), candle animation states (`CandleAnime*` ->
  `CandleAnimeStatus`), loading screens (`CcmLoading` -> `...Normal/Mission`), files (`CFile` ->
  `CStdioFile`, `CFileName`, `CFindFile`), editor/debug leftovers (`IEditObject` -> `CEditRoot`).

## Disc and data files

ISO root: `SLES_538.25`, `SYSTEM.CNF`, `IOPRP300.IMG`, `ZEROIRX.DIL`/`ZEROIRX.HIL` (FF3's IOP module, separate
R3000 binary), `ZERO3.FHD` (file header table), `ZERO3PAL.IML`/`.IMS`, and `IMG_BD.BIN` (2.5 GB data archive,
same name as FF2's). Files are addressed by number (e.g. `LoadReq(0x1979, addr)`, layouts 0x122e..).

- **File numbers have names.** `ZERO3.FHD` lists all 7,724 entries with folder and file name;
  `tools/reverse/fhd.py --header` generates `src/system/eeiop/cd_file_dat.h` (`enum CD_FILE_DAT`, FF2
  cddat.h style: `CAPTION_PHF = 0x11bd`, `PRE_TITLE_PHF = 0x122f`, `LANG_SL_PK4 = 0x1f`, `SYS_BASE_BD_BD =
  0x1a9b`). Language variants follow their base (`CAPTION_PHF + GetLanguage()` -> `F_/G_/S_/I_CAPTION_PHF`).
  52 entries are labels without a file (`TM2ITEM_LABEL_START`, `FURN_START`..`FURN_END`) used as ranges.
  `python fhd.py --find caption` / `--find 0x11bd` looks one up.
- **FHD layout** (little endian): 'FHD\0', version 0x64, size, count, then offsets of five tables indexed
  by file number: unpacked size; extension label (one signed byte per file, GetFileExtLabel);
  (stored size << 1) | compressed; folder/name string offsets (8 bytes); LBA in IMG_BD.BIN (x 0x800);
  then +0x24 use_cd_image (1 on the disc) and two zero words: a 0x30-byte header.
- **Compression**: 6,175 of 7,672 files are compressed, with FF2's CMP container (`CMP_HEADER`: size,
  ext = "LESS", div_size 0x8000, div_num, data at +0x40, block table at +0x20; LZSS blocks). FF3's EE has
  `CMP_Init/CMP_Decode/SlideDecode` (0x271638..0x271c2c, FF2 matches); ReiPan's `system/encodes/cmp.c`
  (from MioPan) decodes it.
- **Data on the host**: the game reads `[paths] data_folder` in
  `%APPDATA%\Mikompilation\ReiPan\reipan.ini` (a folder with
  `IMG_BD.BIN` + `ZERO3.FHD`; `REIPAN_DATA_DIR` overrides it for one run, `build.py --run` passes
  `$REIPAN_DATA` on). The path resolver accepts host names that keep the ISO `;1` suffix
  (`IMG_BD.BIN;1`), and a quoted `data_folder` value. The full EU disc copy
  (`;1` names) is the folder the tools take from `$REIPAN_DATA`; its `bin/data` is a fully unpacked tree
  (containers split into `.b2d`/`.tm2`; `tools/reverse/rawfile.py <FILE>` extracts a file as loaded, CMP-decoded,
  and lists PHF members): not loadable as-is, but the `.tm2` images are reference
  pictures for what a screen should show.
